rukmī dveṣāt mahā balaḥ yācamānāya kaṃsasya preṣyaḥ asau iti cintayan varayām āsa tām rājā bhīṣmakam bhīma vikramam vasoḥ eva tadā vaṃśe damaghoṣaḥ api cedi rāj damaghoṣasya putrāḥ tu pañca bhīma parākramāḥ śiśupālaḥ daśagrīvaḥ raibhyaḥ atha upadiśaḥ balī jñāteḥ samāna vaṃśasya sunītham pradadau sutam jarāsaṃdham puraskṛtya vṛṣṇi śatrum mahā balam kṛtāni āgāṃsi caidyena vṛṣṇīnām tad priya eṣiṇā kṛṣṇa artham vairam abhavat jarāsaṃdhasya vṛṣṇibhiḥ bhīṣmakam varayām āsa sunītha arthe atha rukmiṇīm tām dadau bhīṣmakaḥ ca api śiśupālāya vīryavān tatas caidyam upādāya jarāsaṃdhaḥ narādhipaḥ anuyātaḥ ca pauṇḍreṇa vāsudevena dhīmatā aṅga vaṅga kaliṅgānām īśvaraḥ sa mahā balaḥ mānayiṣyan ca tān rukmī pratyudgamya narādhipān parayā pūjayā upetān ānināya purīm prati pitṛṣu asuḥ priya artham ca rāma kṛṣṇau ubhau api prayayuḥ vṛṣṇayaḥ ca anye rathaiḥ tatra bala anvitāḥ pūjayām āsa pūjā arhān nyavasanta bahis ca te śvas bhāvini vivāhe tu rukmiṇī niryayau bahis catur yujā rathena aindram devatāyatanam śubhā indrāṇīm arcayiṣyantī kṛta kautukamaṅgalā dīpyamānena vapuṣā balena mahatā vṛtā tām dadarśa tatas kṛṣṇaḥ lakṣmīm sākṣāt iva sthitām rūpeṇa agryeṇa saṃpannām devatāyatana antike vahneḥ iva śikhām dīptām māyām bhūmi gatām iva pṛthivīm iva gambhīrām utthitām pṛthivī talāt śriyam agryām iva apadmām bhaviśyām śrī sahāyinīm kṛṣṇena manasā dṛṣṭām durnirīkśyām suraiḥ api śyāma avadātā sā hi āsīt pṛthu cāru āyata īkṣaṇā tāmra oṣṭha nayana apāṅgī pīna ūru jaghana stanī bṛhatī cāru sarva aṅgī tanvī śaśi nibha ānanā tāmra tuṅga nakhī subhrūḥ nīla kuñcita mūrdhajā tīkṣṇa śuklaiḥ samaiḥ dantaiḥ prabhā sadbhiḥ alaṃkṛtā ananyā pramadā loke rūpeṇa yaśasā śriyā tām dṛṣṭvā vavṛdhe kāmaḥ kṛṣṇasya śubha darśanām haviṣā iva analasya arciḥ manaḥ tasyām samādadhat rāmeṇa saha niścitya keśavaḥ su mahā balaḥ tad pramāthe akarot buddhim vṛṣṇibhyaḥ praṇidhāya ca kṛte tu devatā kārye niṣkrāmantīm surālayāt unmathya sahasā kṛṣṇaḥ svam nināya ratha uttamam vṛkṣam utpāṭya rāmaḥ api jaghāna āpatataḥ parān samanahyanta dāśārhāḥ tat ājñāya tu sarvaśas vājibhiḥ vāraṇaiḥ ca api parivavruḥ halāyudham ādāya rukmiṇīm kṛṣṇaḥ jagāma āśu purīm prati rāme ca āsajya tam bhāram yuyudhāne ca vīryavān nivṛtta śatrau vikrānte bhaṅgakāre vidūrathe vṛṣṇi andhakeṣu ca anyeṣu mukhyeṣu madhusūdanaḥ gurum āsajya tam bhāram yayau dvāravatīm prati saṃnaddhāḥ niryayuḥ kruddhāḥ jighāṃsantaḥ janārdanam aṅga vaṅga kaliṅgaiḥ ca sārdham pauṇḍraiḥ ca vīryavān niryayau cedi rājaḥ sa bhrātṛbhiḥ su mahā rathaiḥ tān pratyagṛhṇan saṃrabdhāḥ vṛṣṇi vīrāḥ mahā rathāḥ saṃkarṣaṇam puraskṛtya vāsavam marutaḥ yathā āpatantam hi vegena jarāsaṃdham mahā balam ṣaḍbhiḥ vivyādha nārācaiḥ yuyudhānaḥ mahā mṛdhe tam pratyavidhyat kārūṣaḥ bāṇaiḥ daśabhiḥ āśugaiḥ vipṛthuḥ śiśupālam tu śaraiḥ vivyādha saptabhiḥ aṣṭabhiḥ pratyavidhyat tam śiśupālaḥ pratāpavān gaveśaṇaḥ api caidyam tu ṣaḍbhiḥ vivyādha mārgaṇaiḥ anirdāntaḥ tathā aṣṭābhiḥ bṛhat durgaḥ ca pañcabhiḥ hata aśvam sa ratham tyakvā vipṛthuḥ tu mahā balaḥ āruroha ratham kṣipram bṛhaddurgasya vīryavān vipṛthoḥ sārathiḥ ca api gaveṣaṇa ratham drutam te kruddhāḥ śara varṣeṇa sunītham samavākiran nṛtyantam ratha mārgeṣu cāpa hastāḥ kalāpinaḥ paṭuśam pañcaviṃśatyā vivyādha yudhi mārgaṇaiḥ tatas balī cakradevam bibheda daśabhiḥ śaraiḥ pañcabhiḥ ca api vivyādha saḥ avidūrāt vidūratham vidūrathaḥ api tam ṣaḍbhiḥ vivyādha ājau śitaiḥ śaraiḥ triṃśatā pratyavidhyat tam balī bāṇaiḥ mahā balam kṛtavarmā bibheda ājau rāja putram tribhiḥ śaraiḥ prativivyādha tam kruddhaḥ pauṇḍraḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ śilīmukhaiḥ nivṛtta śatruḥ kāliṅgam bibheda niśitaiḥ śaraiḥ tomareṇa aṃsa deśe tam nirbibheda kaliṅga rāj gajena āsādya kaṅkaḥ tu gajam aṅgasya vīryavān tomareṇa bibheda aṅgam bibheda aṅgaḥ ca tam śaraiḥ kaliṅgasya tathā anīkam nārācaiḥ bibhiduḥ śitaiḥ visṛṣṭena drumeṇa ājau vaṅga rājasya kuñjaram jaghāna rāmaḥ samkruddhaḥ vaṅga rājam ca saṃyuge saṃkarṣaṇaḥ jaghāna ugraiḥ nārācaiḥ kaiśikān bahūn ṣaḍbhiḥ nihatya kārūṣān mahā iṣvāsān sa vīryavān śatam jaghāna saṃkruddhaḥ māgadhānām mahā rathaḥ nihatya tān mahā bāhuḥ jarāsaṃdham tatas abhyayāt tam āpatantam vivyādha nārācaiḥ māgadhaḥ tribhiḥ tam bibheda aṣṭabhiḥ kruddhaḥ nārācaiḥ musala āyudhaḥ gajaiḥ gajāḥ hi saṃkruddhāḥ saṃnipetuḥ sahasraśas rathaiḥ rathāḥ ca saṃrabdhāḥ sādibhiḥ ca api sādinaḥ padātayaḥ padātīn ca śakti carma asi pāṇayaḥ śarāṇām patatām śabdaḥ pakṣiṇām iva śuśruve bherī śaṅkha mṛdaṅgānām veṇūnām ca mṛdhe dhvanim jugūha ghoṣaḥ śastrāṇām jyā ghoṣaḥ ca mahātmanām vaiśampāyanaḥ uvāca kṛṣṇena hriyamāṇām tu rukmī śrutvā tu rukmiṇīm pratijñām akarot kruddhaḥ samakṣam bhīṣmakasya ha krāthaḥ aṃśumān śrutarvā ca veṇudāriḥ ca vīryavān bhīṣmakasya sutaḥ ca api rathena rathinām varaḥ govindam dadṛśuḥ kruddhāḥ saha eva priyayā sthitam sa vivyādha catuḥṣaṣṭyā govindam niśitaiḥ śraiḥ jahāra ca śiraḥ kāyāt sāratheḥ ca asya vīryavān dākṣiṇātyāḥ jighāṃsantaḥ rājānaḥ sarve eva te śrutarvā pañcabhiḥ kruddhaḥ veṇudāriḥ ca saptabhiḥ śrutarvaṇaḥ jaghāna aśvān caturbhiḥ caturaḥ śaraiḥ veṇudāreḥ dhvajam chittvā bhujam vivyādha dakṣiṇam tathā eva ca śrutarvāṇam śaraiḥ vivyādha pañcabhiḥ śiśriye sa dhvajam klāntaḥ nyaṣīdat ca vyathā anvitaḥ muñcantaḥ śara varṣāṇi vāsudevam tatas abhyayus bāṇaiḥ bāṇān ca ciccheda teṣām yudhi janārdanaḥ jaghāna ca eṣām saṃrabdhaḥ yatamānān ca tān śarān kruddhān ādravataḥ vīrān ādravat sa mahā balaḥ sārathim ca asya vivyādha sāyakaiḥ niśitaiḥ tribhiḥ keśavaḥ ca api tam ṣaṣṭyā kruddhaḥ vivyādha mārgaṇaiḥ athā anyat dhanuḥ ādāya rukmī kṛṣṇa jighāṃsayā prāduś cakāra divyāni dīptāni astrāṇi vīryavān astraiḥ astrāṇi saṃvārya tasya kṛṣṇaḥ mhābalaḥ punar ciccheda tam cāpam ratha īṣām ca tribhiḥ śaraiḥ utpapāta rathāt vīraḥ garutmān iva vīryavān visaṃjñaḥ mūrchitaḥ rājā vajreṇa iva hataḥ giriḥ tān ca rājñaḥ śaraiḥ sarvān punar vivyādha keśavaḥ rukmiṇam patitam dṛṣṭvā vyadravanta narādhipāḥ viveṣṭamānam bhūmau tam bhrātaram vīkṣya rukmiṇī pādayoḥ nyapatat bhartuḥ bhrātuḥ jīvita kāṅkṣiṇī tām utthāpya pariṣvajya sāntvayāmāsa keśavaḥ abhayam rukmiṇe dattvā prayayau svām purīm tatas vṛṣṇayaḥ api jarāsaṃdham bhaṅktvā tān ca api pārthivān prayayuḥ dvārakām hṛṣṭāḥ puraskṛtya halāyudham prayāte puṇḍarīkākṣe śrutarvā abhyetya saṃyuge an ānīya svasāram tu rukmī vīrya mada anvitaḥ hīna pratijñaḥ na aicchat sa praveṣṭum kuṇḍinam puram tatra ojasā mahā tejasāḥ saḥ anvaśāt dakṣiṇām diśam bhīṣmakaḥ kuṇḍine ca eva rājā uvāsa mahā manāḥ dvārakām abhisaṃprāpte rāme vṛṣṇi bala anvite rukmiṇyāḥ keśavaḥ pāṇim jagrāha vidhivat prabhuḥ tatas saha tayā reme priyayā prīyamāṇayā sītayā iva purā rāmaḥ paulomī eva puraṃdaraḥ sā hi tasya bhavat jyeṣṭhā patnī kṛṣṇasya bhāminī pativratā guṇa upetā rūpa śīla guṇa anvitā suṣeṇam cāruguptam ca cārubāhum ca vīryavān cārum ca balinām śreṣṭham sutām cārumatīm tathā mahiṣīḥ sapta kalyāṇīḥ tatas anyāḥ madhusūdanaḥ upayeme mahā bāhuḥ guṇa upetāḥ kula udbhavāḥ śaibyām su dattām rūpeṇa śriyā hi apsarasā upamām strī sahasrāṇi ca anyāni ṣoḍaśa atula vikramaḥ upayeme hṛṣīkeśaḥ sarvāḥ bheje sa tāḥ samam parārdhya vastra ābharaṇāḥ kāmaiḥ sarvaiḥ samedhitaḥ jajñire tasya putrāḥ ca tāsu vīrāḥ sahasraśas sarva astra kuśalāḥ sarve balavantaḥ mahā rathāḥ yajvānaḥ puṇya karmāṇaḥ mahābhāgāḥ mahā balāḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca tatas kāle vyatīte tu rukmī mahati vīryavān tatra āhūtāḥ ca rājānaḥ rāja putrāḥ ca rukmiṇā samājagmuḥ mahā vīryāḥ nānā digbhyaḥ śriyā anvitāḥ sā hi tam cakame kanyā sa ca tām śubha locanām śubhāṅgī nāma vaidarbhī kānti dyuti samanvitā pṛthivyām abhavat khyātā rukmiṇaḥ tanayā tadā sa hi sarva astra kuśalaḥ siṃha saṃhananaḥ yuvā vayaḥ rūpa guṇa upetā rāja putrī ca sā abhavat nārāyaṇī candrasenā jāta kāmā ca tam prati vṛtte svayaṃvare jagmuḥ rājānaḥ sva purāṇi te upādāya tu vaidarbhīm pradyumnaḥ dvārakām yayau tadā asya rukmiṇaḥ pautrīm rukmiṇī rukma saṃnibhām patnī artham varayām āsa nāmnā rukmavatī iti sā aniruddham guṇaiḥ dātum kṛta buddhiḥ nṛpaḥ tadā prītyā ca raukmiṇeyasya rukmiṇyāḥ ca api upagrahāt vispardhan api kṛṣṇena vairam tat apahāya saḥ dadāni iti abravīt rājā prītimān janamejaya keśavaḥ saha rukmiṇyā putraiḥ saṃkarṣaṇena ca anyaiḥ ca vṛṣṇibhiḥ sārdham vidarbhān sa balaḥ yayau saṃyuktāḥ jñātayaḥ ca eva rukmiṇaḥ suhṛdaḥ ca ye āhūtāḥ rukmiṇā te api tatra ājagmuḥ narādhipāḥ śubhe tithau mahā rāja nakṣatre ca abhipūjite remire vṛṣṇayaḥ tatra pūjyamānāḥ yathā amarāḥ atha aśmakānām adhipaḥ veṇudāriḥ udāra dhīḥ akṣaḥ śrutarvā cāṇūraḥ krāthaḥ ca eva aṃśumān api jayatsenaḥ kaliṅgānām adhipaḥ ca mahā balaḥ pāṇḍyaḥ ca nṛpatiḥ śrīmān ṛṣīka adhipatiḥ tathā ete saṃmantrya rājānaḥ dākṣiṇātyāḥ mahā ṛddhayaḥ bhavān akṣeṣu kuśalaḥ vayam ca api riraṃsavaḥ priya dyūtaḥ ca rāmaḥ asau akṣeṣu anipuṇaḥ api ca iti uktaḥ rocayām āsa dyūtam rukmī mahā rathaḥ te śubhām kāñcana stambhām kusumaiḥ bhūṣita ajirām sabhām āviviśuḥ hṛṣṭāḥ siktām candana vāriṇā tām praviśya tatas sarve śubhra sraj anulepanāḥ sauvarṇeṣu āsaneṣu āsām cakrire vijigīṣavaḥ bāḍham iti abravīt hṛṣṭaḥ saha dīvyāma paṇya tām nikṛtyā tam jigīṣantaḥ dākṣiṇātyāḥ narādhipāḥ maṇi muktāḥ suvarṇam ca tatra āninyuḥ sahasraśas tatas prāvartata dyūtam teṣām arati nāśanam kalahāya āspadam ghoram durmatīnām kṣaya āvaham niṣkāṇām tu sahasrāṇi suvarṇasya daśa āditas tam jigāya tatas rukmī yatamānam mahā ratham tāvat evā aparam bhūyas baladevam jigāya saḥ asakṛt jīyamānaḥ tu rukmiṇā keśava agrajaḥ suvarṇa koṭim jagrāha glaham tasya mahātmanaḥ jitam iti eva hṛṣṭaḥ atha tam āhvṛtiḥ abhāṣata ślāghamānaḥ ca cikṣepa prahasan musala āyudham avidyaḥ durbalaḥ śrīmān hiraṇyam amitam mayā kaliṅga rājaḥ tat śrutvā prajahāsa bhṛśam tadā dantān vidarśayan hṛṣṭaḥ tatra akrudhyat halāyudhaḥ rukmiṇaḥ ca vacaḥ śrutvā parājaya nimitta jam nigṛhyamāṇaḥ tīkṣṇābhiḥ vāgbhiḥ bhīṣmaka sūnunā roṣam āhārayām āsa jita roṣaḥ api dharma vid saṃkruddhaḥ dharṣaṇām prāpya rauhiṇeyaḥ mahā balaḥ daśa koṭi sahasrāṇi glahaḥ ekaḥ mama aparaḥ etam saṃparigṛhṇīṣva pātaya akṣān narādhipa kṛṣṇa akṣān lohita akṣān ca deśe asmin tvam a pāṃsule iti evam āhvayām āsa rukmiṇam rohiṇīsutaḥ an uktvā vacanam kiṃcid bāḍham iti abravīt punar akṣān rukmī tatas hṛṣṭaḥ pātayām āsa pārthivaḥ cāturakṣe nivṛtte tu nirjitaḥ sa narādhipaḥ satyam āha balaḥ śrīmān dharmeṇa eṣa parājitaḥ manasā samanujñātam tat syāt iti avagamyatām saṃkarṣaṇaḥ tadā utthāya sauvarṇena ūruṇā balī rukmiṇyāḥ bhrātaram jyeṣṭham niṣpipeṣa mahī tale vivāde kupitaḥ rāmaḥ kṣeptāram krūra bhāṣiṇam khaḍgam udyamya tān ca api trāsayām āsa pārthivān stambham sabhāyāḥ sauvarṇam utpāṭya balinām varaḥ nirjagāma sabhā dvārāt trāsayan kratha kaiśikān rukmiṇam nikṛti prajñam sa hatvā yādava ṛṣabhaḥ jagāma śibiram rāmaḥ svam eva sva jana āvṛtaḥ nyavedayata kṛṣṇāya tat ca sarvam yathā bhavat na uvāca sa tadā kiṃcid kṛṣṇaḥ rāmam mahā dyutim nigṛhya ca tadā ātmānam krodhāt aśrūṇi avartayat na hataḥ vāsudevena yaḥ pūrvam para vīra hā aṣṭāpadena balavān rājā vajradhara upamaḥ tasmin hate mahāmātre nṛpatau bhīṣmaka ātmaje vṛṣṇayaḥ ca andhakāḥ ca eva sarve vimanasaḥ abhavan vṛṣṇayaḥ api mahā rāja dhanāni ādāya sarvaśas rāma kṛṣṇau samāśritya yayuḥ dvāravatīm purīm vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca purāṇe nāga rājaḥ asau paṭhyate dharaṇīdharaḥ śeṣaḥ tejaḥ nidhiḥ śrīmān akampyaḥ puruṣottamaḥ yoga ācāryaḥ mahā vīryaḥ su balaḥ balavān balī jarāsaṃdham gadā yuddhe jitavān yaḥ na ca avadhīt anvayuḥ māgadham saṃkhye te ca api vijitāḥ raṇe lāṅgala astram samudyamya brahmadaṇḍa anumantritam tat āghūrṇitam ālakṣya puram duryodhanaḥ nṛpaḥ sa ambam niryātayām āsa sa bhāryam tasya dhīmataḥ āvarjitam iva ābhāti gaṅgām abhimukham nṛpa bhāṇḍīre kathyate rājan yat kṛtam śauriṇā purā dhenukam ca mahā kāyam cikṣepa naga mūrdhani nagaram abhimukhā yat āhṛtā hala vidhṛtā yamunā yamasvasā pratyetya dvārakām viṣṇuḥ hate rukmiṇi vīryavān vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca hayagrīvaḥ nisundaḥ ca vīraḥ pañcajanaḥ tathā nivāsaḥ dhārakā devaiḥ upāyāt upapāditā mahā arṇava parikṣiptā pañca parvata śobhitā tasyām deva pura ābhāyām sabhā kāñcana toraṇā divya gandhaḥ vavau vāyuḥ puṣpa varṣam papāta ha tatas kilakilā śabdaḥ prabhā jāla abhisaṃvṛtaḥ vṛṣṇīn anyān sasvaje ca yathā sthānam yathā vayaḥ pūjitaḥ rāma kṛṣṇābhyām āviveśa sabhām śubhām tatra āsanam alaṃkṛtya sabhām tām sa sureśvaraḥ athā uvāca mahā tejāḥ vāsavaḥ vāsava anujam sāntva pūrvam kareṇa asya saṃspṛṣan vadanam śubham devakīnandana vacaḥ śṛṇu me madhusūdana devānām vipriye nityam ṛṣīṇām ca sa vartate tava ca eva antara prekṣī jahi tam pāpa pūruṣam ayam tvām garuḍaḥ tatra prāpayiṣyati kāma gaḥ iti uktaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ devarājena keśavaḥ tatas saha eva śakreṇa śaṅkha cakra gadā asi bhṛt krameṇa sapta skandhān sa marutām saha vāsavaḥ paśyatām yadu siṃhānām ūrdhvam ācakrame balī vāraṇa indra gataḥ śakraḥ garuḍa sthaḥ janārdanaḥ vidūra sthau prakāśete sūryācandramasau iva stūyamānaḥ yathā śakraḥ krameṇa antaradhīyata samādhāya itikartavyam vāsavaḥ vibudha adhipaḥ svam eva bhavanam prāyāt kṛṣṇaḥ prāgjyotiṣam yayau nānā praharaṇa ākīrṇam tathā ghoram avartata garuḍa sthaḥ mahā bāhuḥ nijaghāna mahā asurān mahā lāṅgala nirbhinnāḥ śara khaṅga nipātitāḥ vineśuḥ dānavāḥ tatra samāsādya janārdanam saṃnikarṣa gatāḥ kecid gatāsu vikṛta ānanāḥ alakāyām virūpākṣam pāpmānam puruṣottamaḥ vibhaktam krakacena iva gireḥ śṛṅgam dvidhā kṛtam vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca vāsava avarajaḥ viṣṇuḥ dadarśa naraka ālayam mahārajata kūṭāni tathā vajrasya saṃcayān jātarūpasya śubhrasya dhārāḥ śata sahasraśas varuṇāt āhṛtam pūrvam narakeṇa iti naḥ śrutam na vai rājñā kubereṇa na śakreṇa yamena ca dānavāḥ hata śiṣṭāḥ ye kośa saṃcaya rakṣiṇaḥ keśavāya mahārhāṇi yāni arhati janārdanaḥ dānavāḥ ūcuḥ rucirābhiḥ patākābhiḥ vasānāḥ vividhāḥ kuthāḥ sa tat sarvam hṛṣīkeśaḥ pratigṛhya parīkṣya ca sarvam āhārayāmāsa dānavaiḥ dvārakām purīm hiraṇya varṣam varṣantam āruroha vihaṃgatam tatra puṇyāḥ vavuḥ vātāḥ hi abhavan ca amalāḥ prabhāḥ maṇīnām hema varṇānām abhibhūya divākaram dadarśa pṛthula śroṇīḥ saṃruddhāḥ giri kandare vrata upavāsa tanu aṅgyaḥ kāṅkṣantyaḥ kṛṣṇa darśanam narakam nihatam jñātvā muram ca eva mahā asuram hayagrīvam nisundam ca tāḥ kṛṣṇam paryavārayan te ca āsām rakṣiṇaḥ vṛddhāḥ dānavāḥ yadu nandanam sarvāsām eva saṃkalpaḥ pati tvena abhavat tatas su priyam bata paśyāmaḥ cira śrutam ariṃdamam tatas tāḥ sāntvayāmāsa pramadāḥ vāsava anujaḥ sarvāḥ kamala patra akṣīḥ dṛṣṭyā vācā ca mādhavaḥ yathārhataḥ sāntvayitvā samābhāṣya ca keśavaḥ yānaiḥ kiṃkara saṃyuktaiḥ uvāha madhusūdanaḥ kiṃkarāṇām sahasrāṇām rakṣasām vāta raṃhasām śibikām vahatām tatra nirghoṣaḥ su mahān abhūt vimala arka indu saṃkāśam maṇi kāñcana toraṇam maṇi parvata śṛṅgam ca sa bhāryam ca janārdanam dikṣu sarvāsu saṃhrādam janayāmāsa pakṣirāj saṃjahāra mahā abhrāṇi vijahāra ca kānicid yayau vāta javaḥ pakṣī janārdana vaśe sthitaḥ deva sadmāni sarvāṇi dadarśa madhusūdanaḥ śakra sadma samāsādya praviveśa janārdanaḥ avatīrya sa tārkṣyāt tu dadarśa vibudha adhipam prītaḥ ca eva abhyanandat tam devarājaḥ śatakratuḥ pradāya kuṇḍale divye vavande tam tadā acyutaḥ sa bhāryaḥ vibudha śreṣṭham nara śreṣṭhaḥ janārdanaḥ dadṛśāte mahātmānau mahābhāgām tapaḥ nvitām tatas te kuṇḍale dattvā vavande tām śacīpatiḥ janārdanam puraskṛtya ca eva śaśaṃśa tat āśirbhiḥ anurūpābhiḥ ubhau abhyavadat tadā paulomī satyabhāmā ca prītyā paramayā yute agṛhṇītām vara arhāyāḥ devyāḥ ca caraṇau śubhau te ca api abhyavadat premṇā deva mātā yaśasvinī evam abhyārcitaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ devamātrā mahā balaḥ devarāja abhyanujñātaḥ ratnaiḥ ca pratipūjitaḥ deva ākrīḍān parikrāman pūjyamānaḥ sura ṛṣibhiḥ sa dadarśa mahā bāhuḥ ākrīḍe vāsavasya ha yam āsādya janaḥ sarvaḥ jātim smarati paurvikīm saṃrakṣyamāṇam devaiḥ tam prasahya amita vikramaḥ utpāṭya āropayāmāsa viṣṇuḥ tam vai mahā drumam saḥ apaśyat satyabhāmām ca divyām apsarasam hariḥ tatas prāyāt dvāravatīm vāyu juṣṭena vai pathā śrutvā tat devarājaḥ tu karma kṛṣṇasya vai tadā anumene mahā bāhuḥ kṛtam karma iti ca abravīt pūjitaḥ devarājena dadṛśe yādavīm purīm tathā karma mahat kṛtvā bhagavān vāsava anujaḥ upāyāt dvārakām viṣṇuḥ śrīmān garuḍa vāhanaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca dadarśa atha purīm kṛṣṇaḥ dvārakām garuḍe sthitaḥ deva sadma pratīkāśām samantāt pratināditām kṛṣṇa priya artham bhūyas tvam kariṣyasi manoharām udyāna vana saṃbādhām dvārakām svarga saṃnibhām kuruṣva vibudha śreṣṭha yathā mama purī tathā tena saṃyujyatām kṣipram purī dvāravatī tvayā kṛṣṇaḥ hi sura kāryeṣu sarveṣu satata utthitaḥ saṃgrāmān ghora rūpān ca vigāhati mahā balaḥ tām indra vacanāt gatvā viśvakarmā purīm tatas tām dadarśa daśārhāṇām īśvaraḥ pakṣivāhanaḥ caya mūrdhni niviṣṭena dyām yathā eva abhra mālayā pūrvasyām diśi lakṣmīvān maṇi kāñcana toraṇaḥ uttarām diśam atyartham vibhūṣayati veṇumān sānavaḥ bhūṣitāḥ tatra keśavasya priya eṣibhiḥ praviṣṭā puṇya salilā bhāvayantī samantataḥ prākāra vara saṃpannām sudhā pāṇḍura lepanām prabhayā abhyabhavat sarvān tān anyān bhāskaraḥ yathā gāndhārī bharata śreṣṭha keśavena niveśitā sūrya prabhaḥ tu prāsādaḥ sarva kāma guṇaiḥ yutaḥ atīva saumyaḥ saḥ api āsīt viṣṭhitaḥ parvataḥ yathā sudattāyāḥ nivāsam tam praśastam sarva daivataiḥ sa śrīmān virajaḥ nāma vyarājat tatra su prabhaḥ tat api utpāṭya kṛṣṇa artham ānītam viśvakarmaṇā tat indra vacanāt tvaṣṭā ānayat kārya hetunā nīyamāne hi tatra āsīt yuddham adbhuta karmaṇaḥ kṛṣṇasya yaḥ abhyarakṣan tam devāḥ pādapam uttamam vihitāḥ vāsudeva artham brahmasthala mahā drumāḥ āhṛtya yadu siṃha artham vihitā viśvakarmaṇā samākula jala upetāḥ pīta śarkara vālukāḥ bahūvuḥ parama upetāḥ tasyām puryām tu pādapāḥ puryām tasyām tu ramyāyām prākāraḥ vai hiraṇmayaḥ vyaktam kiṣku śata utsedhaḥ vihitaḥ viśvakarmaṇā vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca apaśyat sva gṛham kṛṣṇaḥ prāsāda śata śobhitam prāsādaḥ tatra su mahān kṛṣṇa upasthānikaḥ abhavat sphāṭika stambha vidhṛtaḥ vistīrṇaḥ sarva kāñcanaḥ maṇi hema nibhāḥ citrāḥ ratna sopāna bhūṣitāḥ babhūvuḥ parama upetāḥ vāpyaḥ ca vikaca utpalāḥ vyakta kiṣku śata utsedhaḥ parikhā yūtha veṣṭitaḥ cacāra ca yathoddeśam īśvara anucaraḥ hariḥ avatīrya gṛha dvāri kṛṣṇaḥ tu yadu nandanaḥ rāma āhuka gada akrūra pradyumna ādibhiḥ arcitaḥ praviveśa gṛham śauriḥ ādāya maṇi parvatam tam ca śakrasya dayitam pārijātam mahā drumam praveśayām āsa gṛham pradyumnaḥ rukmiṇī sutaḥ te anyonyam dadṛśuḥ bhaumāḥ deha bandhān amānuṣān taiḥ stūyamānaḥ govindaḥ prahṛṣṭaiḥ yādava īśvaraiḥ nyaveśayat ameya ātmā vṛṣṇibhiḥ sahitaḥ acyutaḥ tam ca divyam druma śreṣṭham pārijātam amitra jit anujñāya tatas jñātīn keśavaḥ para vīra hā tāḥ striyaḥ pūjayām āsa saṃkṣiptāḥ narakeṇa yāḥ hāraiḥ candra aṃśu saṃkāśaiḥ maṇibhiḥ ca mahā prabhaiḥ kuṭumbasya īśvarī tu āsīt rukmiṇī bhīṣmaka ātmajā tāsām yathārham harmyāṇi prāsāda śikharāṇi ca ādideśa gṛhān kṛṣṇaḥ paribarhān ca puṣkalān vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca tatas saṃpūjya garuḍam vāsudevaḥ anumānya ca ūrdhvam ācakrame pakṣī yatheṣṭam gaganecaraḥ sa pakṣa vāta saṃkṣubdham samudram makara ālayam kṛṣṇaḥ dadarśa pitaram vṛddham ānakaduṃdubhim ugrasenam ca rājānam baladevam ca mādhavaḥ kāśyam sāṃdīpanim ca eva brahmagārgyam tathā eva ca anyān ca vṛddhān vṛṣṇīnām tān ca bhoja andhakān tathā raṇāt pratinivṛttaḥ ayam akṣataḥ madhusūdanaḥ iti catvara rathyāsu dvāravatyām su pūjitaḥ cākrikaḥ ghoṣayām āsa puruṣaḥ mṛṣṭa kuṇḍalaḥ tatas sāṃdīpanim pūrvam upagamya janārdanaḥ vavande vṛṣṇi nṛpatim āhukam vinaya anvitaḥ āsana agryāṇi viviśuḥ upendra pramukhāḥ tadā sabhām samānayām āsuḥ puruṣāḥ kṛṣṇa śāsanāt tatas sa mānayām āsa dāśārhān yadu sattamān sarvān duṃdubhi śabdena pūjayiṣyan janārdanaḥ tām āsanavatīm ramyām maṇi vidruma toraṇām sudāśārhīm sudāśa arhāḥ viviśuḥ kṛṣṇa śāsanāt tatas puruṣa siṃhaiḥ sā yadubhiḥ sarvatas vṛtā śuśubhe abhyadhikam śubhrā siṃhaiḥ giri guhā yathā rāmeṇa saha govindaḥ kāñcanam mahat āsanam ugrasenam puraskṛtya bheje vṛṣṇi puraskṛtaḥ tatra upaviṣṭān tān vīrān yathāprīti yathāvayas vāsudevaḥ uvāca bhavatām puṇya kīrtīnām tapaḥ bala samādhibhiḥ apadhyānāt ca pāpa ātmā bhaumaḥ sa narakaḥ hataḥ maṇi parvatam utpāṭya śikharam ca etat āhṛtam ayam dhana oghaḥ su mahān kiṃkaraiḥ āhṛtaḥ mayā īśāḥ bhavantaḥ tasya iti tān uktvā virarāma ha tat śrutvā vāsudevasya bhoja vṛṣṇi andhakāḥ vacaḥ jahṛṣuḥ hṛṣṭa lomānaḥ pūjayantaḥ janārdanam ūcuḥ ca enam nṛ vīrāḥ te kṛta añjali puṭāḥ tatas prīyamāṇāḥ sabhām jagmuḥ vāsudeva didṛkṣayā devakī saptamāḥ devyaḥ rohiṇī ca śubha ānanā dadṛśuḥ kṛṣṇam āsīnam rāmam ca eva mahā bhujam tathā kṣaṇa muhūrtābhyām yayā jajñe saha īśvaraḥ putra vat pālyamānāḥ vai vāsudeva ājñayā tadā tām ekām āhuḥ utpannām ekānaṃśā iti mānavāḥ yoga kanyām durādharṣām rakṣā artham keśavasya ca tathā eva sāmaḥ atibalaḥ tām pariṣvajya bhāvinīm rukma padma kara vyagrām śriyam padma ālayām iva upopaviviśuḥ prītāḥ praśaṃsantaḥ adbhutam kṛtam saḥ atha saṃpūjitaḥ pūjyaḥ śūraiḥ taiḥ yadu puṃgavaiḥ kare saṃspṛśya govindam viveśa mahat āsanam sukha upaviṣṭaḥ tān vṛṣṇīn upaviṣṭān uvāca ha āgatam śakra vacanāt jānīdhvam mām nara ṛṣabhāḥ śṛṇudhvam rāja śārdūlāḥ kṛṣṇasya asya parākramam yāni karmāṇi kṛtavān bālyāt prabhṛti keśavaḥ samāśritya jarāsaṃdham śvaśuram kula pāṃsanaḥ jñāti kāryam cikīrṣan tu vasudevaḥ pratāpavān yathā anena śayānena śakaṭa antara cāriṇā rākṣasī nihatā raudrā śakunī veṣa dhāriṇī viṣa digdham stanam kṣudrā prayacchantī mahātmane dadṛśuḥ tām vinihatām rākṣasīm te vanecarāḥ ati adbhutam idam ca āsīt yat śiśuḥ puruṣottamaḥ pādāṅguṣṭhena śakaṭam krīḍamānaḥ vyaloḍayat kāliyaḥ ca mahā nāgaḥ durādharṣaḥ mahā balaḥ pūjyamānaḥ tadā nāgaiḥ divyam vapuḥ adhārayat śīta vāta arditāḥ gāḥ ca dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇena dhīmatā dhṛtaḥ govardhanaḥ śailaḥ sapta rātram mahātmanā śiśunā vāsudevena gavām trāṇa artham icchatā tathā su duṣṭaḥ atibalaḥ mahā kāyaḥ nara anta kṛt dhenukaḥ sa mahā kāyaḥ dānavaḥ su mahā balaḥ nihataḥ vāsudevena gavām trāṇāya durmatiḥ vṛkaiḥ vidrāvayām āsa grahītum samupāgatam tathā vana gataḥ śauriḥ daṃṣṭra āyudha balam hayam pralambaḥ ca mahā kāyaḥ rauhiṇeyena dhīmatā dānavaḥ muṣṭinā ekena kaṃsa amātyaḥ nipātitaḥ vavṛdhāte mahātmānau brahma gārgyeṇa saṃskṛtau janma prabhṛti ca api etau gārgyeṇa parama ṛṣiṇā yāthātathyena vijñāya saṃskāram pratipāditau siṃha śāvau iva udīrṇau mattau haimavatau yathā tatas manāṃsi gopīnām haramāṇau mahā balau āstām goṣṭha carau vīrau deva putra sama dyutī na etau jave vā yuddhe vā krīḍāsu vividhāsu vā nanda gopasya gopālāḥ śekuḥ pratisamīkṣitum vyūḍha uraskau mahā bāhū sāla skandhau iva udgatau nā aśakat ca yadā kaṃsaḥ grahītum bala keśavau kālam mahāntam avasat kṛcchram ānakaduṃdubhiḥ kaṃsaḥ tu pitaram badhvā śūrasenān śaśāsa ha jarāsaṃdham samāśritya tathā evā āhvṛti bhīṣmakau pinākinam samuddiśya kaṃsaḥ cakre narādhipaḥ tatra mallāḥ samāpetuḥ nānādeśyāḥ viśām pate nartakāḥ gāyakāḥ ca eva kuśalāḥ nṛtta sāmasu tatas kaṃsaḥ mahā tejāḥ raṅga vāṭam mahādhanam kuśalaiḥ kārayām āsa śilpibhiḥ sādhu niṣṭhitaiḥ samākīrṇāni adṛśyanta jyotirbhiḥ gaganam yathā bhoja rājaḥ śriyā juṣṭam rāja mañcam mahā ṛddhimat śūraiḥ adhiṣṭhitam kaṃsaḥ sthāpayām āsa vīryavān yadā hi sa mahābhojaḥ rāma kṛṣṇau samāgatau śuśrāva puruṣa vyāghrau sūryācandramasau iva tadā prabhṛti yattaḥ abhūt rakṣām prati narādhipaḥ na ca śete sukham rātrau rāma kṛṣṇau vicintayan śrutvā tu rāma kṛṣṇau ca tam samājam anuttamam ubhau viviśatuḥ vīrau śārdūlau go vrajam yathā tatas praveśe saṃruddhau rakṣibhiḥ puruṣa ṛṣabhau avamṛdya durādharṣau raṅgam viviśatuḥ tadā cāṇūra andhrau viniṣpiṣya keśavena balena ca augraseniḥ sa duṣṭa ātmā sa anujaḥ vinipātitaḥ narakaḥ ca hataḥ bhaumaḥ kuṇḍale ca āhṛte śubhe yajantu bahubhiḥ yajñaiḥ yādavāḥ vīta matsarāḥ devānām su mahat kāryam kṛtam kṛṣṇena dhīmatā yat iṣṭam vaḥ yaduśreṣṭhāḥ kartāsmi tat atandritaḥ bhavatām asmi yūyam ca mama yuṣmāsu aham sthitaḥ iti saṃbodhayan kṛṣṇam abravīt pākaśāsanaḥ mām apraiṣīt suraśreṣṭhaḥ prītāḥ tuṣṭāḥ tathā vayam yatra hrīḥ śrīḥ sthitā tatra yatra śrīḥ tatra saṃnatiḥ saṃnatiḥ hrīḥ tathā śrīḥ ca nityam kṛṣṇe mahātmani nāradaḥ uvāca rukmiṇīm ājahāra āśu keśavaḥ vṛṣṇi puṃgavaḥ hataḥ saubha patiḥ sālvaḥ śaubhaḥ ca dṛḍhadhanvanā vikīrya puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ dyumatsenam apothayat jaghāna yaḥ nara vyāghraḥ rāvaṇasya abhitas carau atha aindraseniḥ ānītaḥ kṛṣṇena adbhuta karmaṇā sahitaḥ sarva yādobhiḥ sāgareṣu mahā balaḥ a cintayitvā deva indram pārijāta drumaḥ hṛtaḥ eṣa ca eka śatam hatvā raṇe rājñām mahātmanām gāndhārīm āvahat dhīmān mahiṣīm priya darśanām sauvīra rājasya sutām prasahya hṛtavān prabhuḥ veṇu dāri kṛte yatnāt jigāya puruṣottamaḥ pūrva dehe jahāra ayam baleḥ tribhuvanam hariḥ yasya na adhigataḥ mṛtyuḥ puram prāgjyotiṣam prati abhibhūtaḥ ca kṛṣṇena sa gaṇaḥ sa mahā balaḥ baleḥ putraḥ mahā vīryaḥ bāṇaḥ draviṇavattaraḥ pīṭham tathā mahā bāhuḥ kaṃsa amātyam janārdanaḥ nirjitya puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ preṣayām āsa sāgaram saṃjīvayām āsa mṛtam putram sāṃdīpaneḥ tathā devamātuḥ dadau ca api prīti artham vajrapāṇinaḥ saṃsthāpya dharmān martyeṣu yajñaiḥ iṣṭvā āpta dakṣiṇaiḥ bahu ratna samākīrṇā caitya yūpa śata aṅkitā stavyaḥ ayam evam śataśas tathā śata sahasraśas antaḥ hi karmaṇām asya dṛṣṭa pūrvaḥ na kenacid evam etāni karmāṇi śiśuḥ madhyavayāḥ tathā iti uvāca purā vyāsaḥ tapaḥ dīrgheṇa cakṣuṣā iti saṃstūya govindam mahā indra vacanāt ṛṣiḥ tatas tat vasu govindaḥ dideśa andhaka vṛṣṇiṣu yajñaiḥ iṣṭvā mahātmānaḥ dvārakām āvasan purīm bahūnām strī sahasrāṇām aṣṭau bhāryāḥ prakīrtitāḥ tāsām apatyān yaṣṭānām bhagavān prabravītu me vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca aṣṭau mahiṣyaḥ putriṇyaḥ iti prādhānyataḥ smṛtāḥ subhīmā ca tathā mādrī raukmiṇeyān imān śṛṇu dvitīyaḥ cārudeṣṇaḥ ca vṛṣṇi siṃhaḥ mahā rathaḥ cārubhadraḥ bhadracāruḥ sudaṃṣṭraḥ drumaḥ eva ca suṣeṇaḥ cāruguptaḥ ca cāruvindaḥ ca cārumān sudattāyāḥ tu śaibyāyāḥ saṃgrāmajit ajāyata subhīmāyāḥ sutaḥ mādryāḥ vṛkāśvaḥ vṛka nirvṛtiḥ kumāraḥ vṛkadīptiḥ ca lakṣmaṇāyāḥ prajāḥ śṛṇu gātravān gātraguptaḥ ca gātravindaḥ ca vīryavān aśrutaḥ ca sutaḥ jajñe kālindyāḥ śruta sattamaḥ aśrutam śrutasenāyai pradadau madhusūdanaḥ tam pradāya hṛṣīkeśaḥ tām bhāryām muditaḥ abravīt eṣa vām ubhayoḥ astu dāyādaḥ śāśvatīḥ samāḥ bṛhatyām tu gadasya āhuḥ śaibyāyām aṅgadam sutam utpannam kumudam ca eva śvetam śvetām tathā aṅganām kauśikyām sutasomāyām yaudhiṣṭhiryām yudhiṣṭhirau kāpālī garuḍaḥ ca eva jajñāte citra yodhinau bhrātarau deva saṃkāśau ubhau puruṣa sattamau narācyām kapilaḥ jajñe pauṇḍraḥ ca sutanoḥ sutaḥ jarāḥ nāma niṣādānām prabhuḥ sarva dhanuṣmatām śineḥ tu satya vāc jajñe satyakaḥ ca mahā rathaḥ satyakasya ātmajaḥ śūraḥ yuyudhānaḥ tu ajāyata bhūmeḥ yugaṃdharaḥ putraḥ iti vaṃśaḥ samāpyate yaḥ eṣa bhavatā pūrvam śambara ghnā iti udāhṛtaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca rukmiṇyām vāsudevasya lakṣmī kāmaḥ dhṛta vrataḥ śambara anta karaḥ jajñe pradyumnaḥ kāmadarśanaḥ tam sapta rātre saṃpūrṇe niśīthe sūtikāgṛhāt jahāra kṛṣṇasya sutam śiśum vai kālaśambaraḥ viditam tat ca kṛṣṇasya deva māyā anuvartinaḥ sa mṛtyunā parīta āyuḥ māyayā abhijahāra tam dorbhyām utkṣipya nagaram svam jagāma mahā asuraḥ anapatyā tu tasya āsīt bhāryā rūpa guṇa anvitā nāmnā māyāvatī nāma māyā iva śubha darśana tasyāḥ mahiṣyāḥ nāthinyāḥ dānavaḥ kāla coditaḥ sā tam saṃvardhayām āsa kārṣṇim kamala locanam māyāḥ ca asmai dadau sarvāḥ dānavīḥ kāma mohitā tam sā māyāvatī kāntam kāmayām āsa kāminī iṅgitaiḥ ca api vīkṣantī prālobhayata sa smitā prasajjantīm tu tām devīm babhāṣe cāru hāsinīm aho duṣṭa svabhāvā asi strī tvena cala mānasā nanu te aham sutaḥ saumye kaḥ ayam śīla vyatikramaḥ vidyut saṃpāta capalaḥ svabhāvaḥ khalu yoṣitām yadi te aham sutaḥ saumye yadi vā na ātmajaḥ śubhe evam uktā tu sā bhīruḥ kāmena vyathita indriyā na tvam mama sutaḥ saumya nā api te śambaraḥ pitā rūpavān asi vikrāntaḥ tvam jātyā vṛṣṇi nandanaḥ sūtikāgāra madhyāt tvam śiśuḥ uttāna śāyitaḥ pākaśāsana kalpasya hṛtaḥ tvam śambareṇa ha sā ca te karuṇam mātā tvām bālam anuśocatī atyartham śrāmyate vīra vivatsā saurabhī yathā sa hi śakrāt api mahān pitā te garuḍadhvajaḥ iha tvām nā abhijānāti bālam eva apavāhitam kānta vṛṣṇi kumāraḥ tvam na hi tvam śambara ātmajaḥ vīra nā evaṃvidhān putrān dānavāḥ janayanti hi tatas aham kāmayāmi tvām na hi tvam janitaḥ mayā rūpam te saumya paśyantī sīdāmi hṛdi durbalā śrutvā etat nikhilam sarvam māyāvatyā prabhāṣitam cakra āyudha ātmajaḥ kruddhaḥ śambaram sa samāhvayat samasta māyā māyā jñaḥ vikrāntaḥ samare avyayaḥ aṣṭamyām nihataḥ yuddhe māyayā kālaśambaraḥ gṛhya māyāvatīm devīm svām agacchat purīm pituḥ saḥ antarikṣa gataḥ bhūtvā māyayā śīghra vikramaḥ ājagāma purīm ramyām rakṣitām tejasā pituḥ saḥ antarikṣāt prapatitaḥ keśava antaḥpure śiśuḥ māyāvatyā saha tayā rūpavān iva manmathaḥ tasmin tatra avapatite mahiṣyaḥ keśavasya yāḥ vismitāḥ ca eva hṛṣṭāḥ ca bhīrāḥ ca eva abhavan tadā tatas tam kāma saṃkāśam kāntayā saha saṃgatam prekṣantyaḥ hṛṣṭa vadanāḥ pibantyaḥ nayana āsavam tam vrīḍita mukham dṛṣṭvā sajjamānam pade pade abhavan snigdha saṃkalpāḥ prahṛṣṭāḥ kṛṣṇa yoṣitaḥ rukmiṇī tu eva tam dṛṣṭvā śoka ārtā putra gṛddhinī sapatnī śata saṃkīrṇā sa bāṣpā vākyam abravīt dhanyāyāḥ khalu ayam putraḥ dīrgha āyuḥ priya darśanaḥ idṛśaḥ kāma saṃkāśaḥ yauvane prathame sthitaḥ jīva putrā tvayā putra kā sā bhāgya vibhūṣitā kim artham ca ambu daśyāma sa bhāryaḥ tvam iha āgataḥ bhavet yadi na nītaḥ syāt kṛtāntena balīyasā vyaktam vṛṣṇi kumāraḥ ayam na mithyā mama tarkitam vijñātaḥ asi mayā cihnaiḥ vinā cakram janārdanaḥ mukham nārāyaṇasya iva keśāḥ keśāntaḥ eva ca kaḥ tvam vṛṣṇi kulam sarvam vapuṣā dyotayan sthitaḥ saḥ apaśyat tam sutam jyeṣṭham siddham manmatha lakṣaṇaiḥ snuṣām māyāvatīm ca eva hṛṣṭa cetāḥ janārdanaḥ saḥ abravīt sahasā devīm rukmiṇīm devatām iva ayam te devi saṃprāptaḥ putraḥ cāpa dharaḥ prabhuḥ anena śambaram hatvā māyā yuddha viśāradam hatāḥ māyāḥ ca tāḥ sarvāḥ yābhiḥ devān abādhata satī ca iyam śubhā sādhvī bhāryā vai tanayasya te māyāvatī iti vikhyātā śambarasya gṛha uṣitā manmathe tu gate nāśam gate ca anaṅga tām purā kāma patnī hi kanyā eṣā kāma kāmā ratiḥ śubhā māyā rūpeṇa tam daityam mohayati asakṛt śubhā na ca eṣā tasya kaumāre vaśe tiṣṭhati śobhanā rūpam kṛtvā śambaram āviśat patnī eṣā mama putrasya snuṣā tava vara aṅganā praveśaya enām bhavanam pūjyā hi eṣā snuṣā mama vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca hṛtaḥ yadā eva pradyumnaḥ śambareṇa ātmaghātinā tam eva māsam sāmbaḥ tu jāmbavatyām ajāyata bālyāt prabhṛti rāmeṇa māneṣu viniyojitaḥ rāmāt anantaram ca eva mānitaḥ sarva vṛṣṇibhiḥ jāta mātre tatas kṛṣṇaḥ śubham tat puram āviśat nihata amitra sāmantaḥ śakra udyānam yathā amaraḥ yādavīm ca śriyam dṛṣṭvā svām śriyam dveṣṭi vāsavaḥ janārdana bhayāt ca eva na śāntim lebhire nṛpāḥ tām śrutvā mādhavīm lakṣmīm sa putram ca janārdanam purīm dvārāvatīm ca eva niviṣṭām sāgara antare dūtaiḥ taiḥ kṛta saṃdhānāḥ pṛthivyām sarva pārthivāḥ śriyam draṣṭum hṛṣīkeśam ājagmuḥ kṛṣṇa mandiram duryodhana mukhāḥ sarve dhṛtarāṣṭra vaśa anugāḥ pāṇḍava pramukhāḥ ca eva dhṛṣṭadyumna ādayaḥ nṛpāḥ pāṇḍya cola kaliṅga īśāḥ vāhlīkāḥ draviḍāḥ śakāḥ akṣauhiṇīḥ prakarṣantaḥ daśa ca aṣṭau ca bhūmipāḥ ājagmuḥ yādava purīm govinda bhuja pālitām te parvatam raivatakam parivārya avanīśvarāḥ viviśuḥ yojana āḍhyāsu svāsu svāsu avanīṣu atha tatas śrīmān hṛṣīkeśaḥ saha yādava puṃgavaiḥ samīpam mānava indrāṇām niryayau kamala īkṣaṇaḥ sa teṣām naradevānām madhye madhuniṣūdanaḥ vyarājata yadu śreṣṭhaḥ śaradi iva nabhaḥ śaśī sa tatra samudācāram yathāsthānam yathāvayas kṛtvā siṃhāsane kṛṣṇaḥ kāñcane niṣasāda ha rājānaḥ api yathāsthānam niṣeduḥ vividheṣu atha siṃhāsaneṣu citreṣu pīṭheṣu ca janādhipāḥ sa yādava narendrāṇām samājaḥ śuśubhe tadā teṣām citrāḥ kathāḥ tatra pravṛttāḥ tad samāgame yadūnām pārthivānām ca keśavasya upaśṛṇvataḥ saṃveṣṭita jaṭā bhāraḥ vīṇā saktena bāhunā sa papāta narendrāṇām madhye pāvaka varcasām saḥ avagāhya narendrāṇām madhyam sāgara saṃnibham āsana stham yaduśreṣṭam uvāca muniḥ avyayaḥ āścaryam khalu devānām ekaḥ tvam puruṣottama dhanyaḥ ca asi mahā bāho loke na anyaḥ asti kaḥ cana evam uktaḥ smitam kṛtvā pratyuvāca munim prabhuḥ āścaryaḥ ca eva dhanyaḥ ca dakṣiṇābhiḥ saha iti aham tam prasthitam abhiprekṣya pārthivāḥ prāhuḥ īśvaram dakṣiṇābhiḥ saha iti evam pratyukte api ca nārade tān uvāca tatas kṛṣṇaḥ sarvān pārthiva puṃgavān brūhi nārada tattva artham śrāvyantām pṛthivīkṣitaḥ yat tvayā abhihitam vākyam mayā ca pratibhāṣitam sa pīṭhe kāñcane śubhre su upaviṣṭaḥ su alaṃkṛtaḥ śruyatām bho nṛpa śreṣṭhāḥ yāvantaḥ stha samāgatāḥ aham kadācid gaṅgāyāḥ tīre triṣavaṇa atithiḥ carāmi ekaḥ kṣapā apāye saṃdṛśyati divākare apaśyam aśma kūṭa ābham kapāla dvaya dehinam krośa maṇḍala vistāram tāvat vṛttam samantataḥ catur caraṇa saṃśliṣṭam klinnam śaivala paṅkilam mama vīṇā ākṛtim kūrmam gaja carma caya upamam saḥ aham tam pāṇinā spṛṣṭvā proktavān jalacāriṇam tvam āścarya śarīraḥ asi kūrma dhanyaḥ ca me mataḥ yaḥ tvam evam abhedyābhyām kapālābhyām samāvṛtaḥ sa mām uvāca ambu caraḥ kūrmaḥ mānuṣa vat svayam kim āścaryam mayi mune dhanyaḥ ca aham katham vibho gaṅgā iyam nimnagā dhanyā kim āścaryam atas param saḥ aham kutūhala āviṣṭaḥ nadīm gaṅgām upasthitaḥ dhanyā asi tvam nadī śreṣṭhe nityam āścarya darśane yā tvam evam mahā dehaiḥ śvāpadaiḥ upaśobhitā hradinī sāgaram yāsi rakṣantī tāpasa ālayān evam uktā tatas gaṅgā rūpiṇī pratyabhāṣata na aham dhanyā dvijaśreṣṭha na api āścarya upaśobhitā tava satye niviṣṭasya vākyam mām pratibādhate yatra aham iva vistīrṇāḥ śataśas yānti nimnagāḥ saḥ aham tripathagā vākyam śrutvā arṇavam upasthitaḥ āścaryaḥ khalu lokānām dhanyaḥ ca asi mahā arṇava sthāne tvā vāri vāhinyaḥ saritaḥ loka bhāvanāḥ ṛte tu pṛthivīm loke kim āścaryam atas param saḥ aham sāgara vākyena kṣitim kṣiti tale sthitaḥ āścaryā ca asi bhūteṣu mahatyā kṣamayā yute tena khalu asi lokānām dharaṇī manuja araṇiḥ kṣamā tvattaḥ prasūtā ca karma ca ambara gāminām na asmi dhanyā na ca āścaryā pārakyā iyam dhṛtiḥ mama eṣu āścaryāṇi dṛśyante ete lokasya setavaḥ dhanyāḥ dṛśyante bahu āścaryāḥ ca bhūdharāḥ kāñcanasya agra ratnasya dhātūnām ca viśeṣataḥ mama tu etat vacaḥ śrutvā parvatāḥ tasthuṣām varāḥ ūcuḥ mām sāntva yuktāni vacāṃsi vana śobhitāḥ brahmarṣe na vayam dhanyāḥ na api āścaryāṇi santi naḥ brahmā prajāpatiḥ dhanyaḥ sa ca āścaryaḥ sureṣu api pāraṃparyāt upagataḥ praṇāma avanata ānanaḥ saḥ aham vākya samāpti artham śrāvayāmi ātmayoninam sa deva dānavāḥ martyāḥ loke bhūta indriya ātmakāḥ bhavanti sarva deva īśa dṛśyam sarvam idam vapuḥ teṣām eva asi yat sraṣṭā lokānām api saṃbhavaḥ dhanya āścarya āśritaiḥ vākyaiḥ kim mām nārada bhāṣase āścaryam paramam devāḥ dhanyāḥ devāḥ ca nārada ṛk sāma yajuṣām satyam atharvaṇi ca yat matam parameṣṭhyena vākyena coditaḥ aham svayaṃbhuvā veda upasthānikām cakre matim saṃkrānta vistarām uvāca ca enān caturaḥ mantra pravacana arcitān dhanyāḥ bhavantaḥ puṇyāḥ ca nityam āścarya viśrutāḥ ādhārāḥ ca eva viprāṇām evam āha prajāpatiḥ svayaṃbhuvaḥ api iha param bhavatsu praśnaḥ āhitaḥ yuṣmat parataram na asti śrutyāḥ vā tapasā api vā pratūcuḥ te tatas vākyam devāḥ mām abhitas sthitāḥ yajña arthe tu vayam sṛṣṭāḥ sṛṣṭāḥ yena sma nārada svayaṃbhuvaḥ api iha parā vedānām ca parā gatiḥ tatas aham abruvam yajñān gṛhastha agni puraskṛtān bho yajñāḥ paramam tejaḥ yuṣmāsu khalu lakṣyate dhanyāḥ khalu bhavantaḥ ye dvijātīnām sva vaṃśinaḥ tena khalu agnayaḥ tṛptim puṣmābhiḥ yānti tarpitāḥ bhāgaiḥ ca tridaśāḥ sarve mantraiḥ ca eva mahā ṛṣayaḥ agniṣṭoma ādayaḥ yajñāḥ mama vākyāt anantaram pratyūcuḥ mām param vākyam sarve yūpadhvajāḥ sthitāḥ āścarya śabdaḥ nā asmāsu dhanya śabdaḥ api vā mune āścaryam paramam viṣṇuḥ sa hi asmākam parā gatiḥ yat ājyam vayam aśnīmaḥ hutam agniṣu pāvanam tat sarvam puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ loka mūrtiḥ prayacchati dṛṣṭaḥ ca ayam mayā viṣṇuḥ bhavadbhiḥ abhisaṃvṛtaḥ yat mayā abhihitaḥ hi eṣa tvam āścaryam janārdana dhanyaḥ ca asi iti bhavatām madhya sthaḥ hi atra pārthivāḥ dakṣiṇābhiḥ saha iti evam paryāptam vacanam mama yajñānām hi gatiḥ viṣṇuḥ sarveṣām saha dakṣiṇaḥ dakṣiṇābhiḥ saha iti evam praśnaḥ mama samāptavān kūrmeṇa abhihitam pūrvam pāraṃparyāt iha āgatam sa dakṣiṇe asmin puruṣe tat vākyam paryaya āgatam yat mām bhavantaḥ pṛcchanti vākyasya asya vinirṇayam nārade tu gate svargam sarve te pṛthivīkṣitaḥ vismitāḥ svāni rāṣṭrāṇi jagmuḥ sa bala vāhanāḥ punar eva mahā bāho kṛṣṇasya jagatīpateḥ karmaṇām anusaṃdhānam purāṇasya mahātmanaḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca gāṇḍīvadhanvā bībhatsuḥ māhātmyam keśavasya yat yudhiṣṭhiram jita amitram iti tat śṛṇu kaurava purā aham dvārakām yātaḥ saṃbandhīn avalokakaḥ tatas kadācid dharma ātmā dīkṣitaḥ madhusūdanaḥ kṛṣṇam vijñāpayām āsa paritrāhi iti ca abravīt brāhmaṇaḥ uvāca rakṣā adhikāraḥ bhavataḥ parirakṣasva mām vibho vāsudevaḥ uvāca na bhetavyam dvijaśreṣṭha rakṣāmi tvām kutas anagha brāhmaṇaḥ uvāca trayaḥ hṛtāḥ caturtham tvam kṛṣṇa rakṣitum arhasi brāhmaṇyāḥ sūti kālaḥ adya tatra rakṣā vidhīyatām rakṣā ca brāhmaṇe kāryā sarva avasthā gataiḥ api śrutvā aham evam kṛṣṇasya tatas avocam narottama mām niyojaya govinda rakṣiṣyāmi dvijam bhayāt iti uktaḥ sa smitam kṛtvā mām uvāca janārdanaḥ tatas mām vrīḍitam jñātvā punar āha janārdanaḥ gamyatām kaurava śreṣṭha śakyate yadi rakṣitum ṛte rāmam mahā bāhuḥ pradyumnam ca mahā balam tam agratas dvijam kṛtvā prayātaḥ saha senayā su muhūrtena tu vayam tam grāmam prāpya bhārata viśrānta vāhanāḥ sarve vāsāya upagatāḥ tadā tatas śakunayaḥ dīptāḥ mṛgāḥ ca krūra bhāṣiṇaḥ dīptāyām diśi vāśantaḥ bhayam āvedayanti me saṃdhyārāgaḥ japāvarṇaḥ bhānumān ca eva niṣprabhaḥ papāta mahatī ca ulkā pṛthivī ca api akampata yuyudhāna purogāḥ ca vṛṣṇi andhaka mahā rathāḥ sarve yukta rathāḥ sajjāḥ svayam ca aham tathā abhavam kālaḥ ayam samanuprāptaḥ brāhmaṇyāḥ prasavasya me muhūrtāt iva ca aśrauṣam kṛpaṇam rudita svanam atha ākāśe punar vācam aśrauṣam bālakasya vai hāhā iti hriyamāṇasya na ca paśyāmi rākṣasam viṣṭambhitāḥ diśaḥ sarvāḥ hṛtaḥ eva sa bālakaḥ brahmaṇaḥ ārta svaram kṛtvā hṛte tasmin kumārake vācaḥ sa paruṣāḥ tīvrāḥ śrāvayām āsa mām tadā vṛṣṇayaḥ hata saṃkalpāḥ tathā aham naṣṭa cetanaḥ mām eva hi viśeṣeṇa brāhmaṇaḥ paryabhāṣata rakṣiṣyāmi iti ca uktam te na ca rakṣasi me sutam śṛṇu vākyam idam śeṣam yat tvam arhasi durmate vṛthā tvam spardhase nityam kṛṣṇena amita buddhinā pāpasya api tathā mūḍha bhāgam prāpnoti arakṣitā rakṣiṣyāmi iti ca uktam te na ca śaknoṣi rakṣitum mogham gāṇḍīvam etat te mogham vīryam yaśaḥ ca te saha vṛṣṇi andhaka sutaiḥ yatra kṛṣṇaḥ mahā dyutiḥ tatas dvāravatīm gatvā dṛṣṭvā madhunighātinam vrīḍitaḥ śoka saṃtaptaḥ govindena upalakṣitaḥ sa tu mām vrīḍitam dṛṣṭvā samāśvasya ca mādhavaḥ yojaya aśvān iti tadā dārukam pratyabhāṣata āropya brāhmaṇam kṛṣṇaḥ tu avaropya ca dārukam mām uvāca tatas śauriḥ sārathyam kriyatām iti tatas samāsthāya ratham kṛṣṇaḥ aham brāhmaṇaḥ sa ca prayātāḥ sma diśam saumyām udīcīm kaurava ṛṣabha tvayā pravartite gādhe yāsyāmi gamanīya tām anye api evam gamiṣyanti rājānaḥ darpa mohitāḥ vāsudevaḥ uvāca mām ṛte na pumān kaścid anyaḥ tvām dharṣayiṣyati abhiśāpa bhayāt bhītaḥ bāḍham evam bhaviṣyati śoṣayāmi eṣa mārgam te yena tvam tāta yāsyasi vāsudevaḥ uvāca mayā datta varaḥ pūrvam na śoṣam tvam iha arhasi mānuṣāḥ te na jānīyuḥ vividhān ratna saṃcayān jalam stambhaya sādho tvam tatas yāsyāmi aham rathī na hi kaścid pramāṇam te ratnānām vetsyate naraḥ mahā meruḥ sa kailāsaḥ indrakūṭaḥ ca nāmataḥ varṇa rūpāṇi bibhrantaḥ vividhāni adbhutāni ca te kṛṣṇasya vacaḥ śrutvā pratigṛhya ca parvatāḥ asaktam ca rathaḥ yāti megha jāleṣu iva aṃśumān tatas kadā cit duḥkhena ratham ūhuḥ turaṃgamāḥ paṅka bhūtam hi timiram sparśāt vijñāyate anagha tat āsādya hayāḥ rājan niṣprayatnāḥ tatas sthitāḥ tatas cakreṇa govindaḥ pāṭayitvā tu tat tamaḥ ākāśam darśayām āsa ratha panthānam ūttamam niṣkramya tamasaḥ tasmāt ākāśe darśite tadā bhaviṣyāmi iti saṃjñā me bhayam ca vigatam mama tam praviṣṭaḥ hṛṣīkeśaḥ dīptam tejaḥ nidhim tadā sa muhūrtāt tatas kṛṣṇaḥ niścakrāma tadā prabhuḥ caturaḥ bālakān gṛhya brāhmaṇasya ātmajān tadā pradadau brāhmaṇāya atha putrān sarvān janārdanaḥ trayaḥ pūrvam hṛtāḥ ye ca sadyojātaḥ ca bālakaḥ prahṛṣṭaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ tāta putrān dṛṣṭvā punar prabho aham ca parama prītaḥ vismitaḥ ca abhavam nṛpa tatas vayam punar sarve brāhmaṇasya ca te sutāḥ tatas sma dvārakām prāptāḥ kṣaṇena nṛpa sattama sa putram bhojayitvā tam dvijam kṛṣṇaḥ mahā yaśāḥ dhanena tarpayitvā ca gṛham prāsthāpayat tadā tatas kṛṣṇaḥ bhojayitvā śatāni su bahūni ca viprāṇām ṛṣi kalpānām kṛtakṛtyaḥ abhavat tadā tatas saha mayā bhuktvā vṛṣṇi bhojaiḥ ca sarvaśas vicitrāḥ ca kathāḥ divyāḥ kathayām āsa bhārata apṛccham tat yathāvṛttam kṛṣṇam yat dṛṣṭvān aham parvatānām ca vivaram kṛtam te katham acyuta kim artham tena te bālāḥ tadā ca apahṛtāḥ prabho katham ca alpena kālena kṛtam naḥ tat gatāgatam vāsudevaḥ uvāca mad darśana artham te bālāḥ hṛtāḥ tena mahātmanā prakṛtiḥ sā mama parā vyaktā avyaktā ca bhārata sā sāṃkhyānām gatiḥ pārtha yoginām ca tapasvinām aham te parvatāḥ sapta ye dṛṣṭvā vividhāḥ tvayā aham tamaḥ ghanībhūtam aham eva ca pāṭakaḥ aham ca kālaḥ bhūtānām dharmaḥ ca aham prakīrtitaḥ catasraḥ ca diśaḥ sarvāḥ mama eva ātmā caturvidhaḥ vāsudevaḥ uvāca vetsyase mām yathātattvam tat vyākhyāsyāmi te anagha ṛṣayaḥ devatāḥ yajñāḥ mat tejaḥ bharata ṛṣabha candra ādityau ahorātre pakṣāḥ māsāḥ tathā kṣapāḥ mantrāḥ ca vividhāḥ pārtha yāni śāstrāṇi kānicid mad mayam viddhi kaunteya kṣayam sṛṣṭim ca bhārata evam uktaḥ asmi kṣṛṇena prīyatā bharata ṛṣabha vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca pūjayām āsa manasā govindam puruṣottamam rājabhiḥ ca tathā āsannaiḥ ye tatra āsan samāgatāḥ bhūyas eva dvijaśreṣṭha yadu siṃhasya dhīmataḥ yāni aham vividhāni asya śrutvā prīye mahā mune prabrūyāḥ sarvaśas tāta tāni me vaktum arhasi vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca karmāṇi uktāni vai rājan bhūyas ca eva śrutāni te kathitāni mayā sādho na antam śakyam hi karmaṇām gantum bharata śārdūla vistarasya mahātmanaḥ viṣṇoḥ atula vīryasya prathita udāra karmaṇaḥ dvāravatyām nivasatā yadu siṃhena dhīmatā rāṣṭrāṇi nṛpa mukhyānām kṣobhitāni mahīkṣitām puram prāgjyotiṣam gatvā punar tena mahātmanā samudra madhye duṣṭa ātmā narakaḥ dānavaḥ hataḥ vāsavam ca raṇe jitvā pārijātaḥ hṛtaḥ balāt dantavakraḥ ca kārūṣaḥ nihataḥ dakṣiṇāpathe śiśupālaḥ ca saṃpūrṇe kilbiṣa eka śate hataḥ gatvā ca śoṇitapuram śaṃkareṇa abhirakṣitaḥ baleḥ sutaḥ mahā vīryaḥ bāṇaḥ bāhu sahasravān mahā mṛdhe mahā rāja jitvā jīvan visarjitaḥ nirjitāḥ pāvakāḥ ca eva giri madhye mahātmanā vikṣobhya sāgaram sarvam pāñcajanyaḥ vaśīkṛtaḥ hayagrīvaḥ ca nihataḥ nṛpāḥ ca anye mahā balāḥ rathena jitvā nṛpatīn gāndhāra tanayā hṛtā bhraṣṭa rājyāḥ ca śoka ārtāḥ pāṇḍavāḥ parirakṣitāḥ dāhitam ca vanam ghoram puruhūtasya khāṇḍavam gāṇḍīvam ca agninā dattam arjunāya upapāditam kuntyāḥ ca pramukhe proktā pratijñā pāṇḍavān prati mokṣitaḥ ca mahā tejāḥ nṛgaḥ śāpāt su dāruṇāt yavanaḥ ca hataḥ saṃkhye kālaḥ iti abhiviśrutaḥ vijitau yudhi durdharṣau jāmbavān ca parājitaḥ sāṃdīpaneḥ tathā putraḥ tava ca eva pitā nṛpa saṃgrāmāḥ bahavaḥ ca eva ghorāḥ nara vara kṣayāḥ nihatāḥ ca nṛpāḥ sarve kṛtvā tat rūpam adbhutam tvattaḥ śrutavatām śreṣṭha vāsudevasya dhīmataḥ yat ca tat kathitam pūrvam bāṇam prati mahā asuram vāsudevena sa katham bāṇaḥ saṃkhye parājitaḥ saṃrabdhaḥ dvaṃdva yuddha arthī jīvan muktaḥ katham ca saḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca manuṣya loke bāṇena yathā abhūt vigrahaḥ mahān bali putraḥ raṇa ślāghī jitvā jīvan visarjitaḥ nityam sāṃnidhya tā ca eva gāṇapatyam tathā akṣayam asakṛt nirjitāḥ devāḥ sa sādhyāḥ sa marut gaṇāḥ te parājaya saṃtrastāḥ nirāśāḥ mad parājaye nāka pṛṣṭham upāgamya nivasanti yathāsukham saḥ aham nirāśaḥ yuddhasya jīvitam na adya kāmaye a yudhyataḥ vṛthā hi eṣām bāhūnām dhāraṇam mama bhavitā bāṇa yuddham te tathā tat śṛṇu dānava iti evam uktaḥ prahasan bāṇaḥ su bahuśas mudā diṣṭyā bāhu sahasrasya na vṛthā dhāraṇam mama diṣṭā sahasrākṣam aham vijetā punar āhave ānanda ja aśru pūrṇābhyām netrābhyām ari mardanaḥ pañca añjali śataiḥ devam pūjayan patitaḥ bhuvi uttiṣṭha uttiṣṭha bāhūnām ātmanaḥ sva kulasya ca sadṛśam prātsyase vīre yuddham apratimam raṇe harṣeṇa abhyutthitaḥ śīghram natvā sa vṛṣabhadhvajam śitikaṇṭha visṛṣṭaḥ tu bāṇaḥ para puraṃjayaḥ tatra upaviṣṭaḥ prahasan kumbhāṇḍam idam abravīt kaccit trailokya rājyam te vyādiṣṭam śṛlapāṇinā kaccit viṣṇu paritrāsam vimokṣyanti diteḥ sutāḥ kaccit śārṅga gadā pāṇeḥ sthitasya param āhave na viṣṇoḥ bhaya saṃtrāsāt prayāsyanti diśaḥ asurāḥ pātāla vāsam utsṛjya kaccit tava bala āśrayāt vibudha āvāsa niratāḥ bhaviṣyanti mahā asurāḥ baliḥ viṣṇu bala ākrāntaḥ baddhaḥ tava pitā nṛpa kaccintrabhiḥ kramaiḥ pūrvam hṛtān lokān imān prabho punar pratyānayiṣyāmaḥ jitvā sarvān divaukasaḥ kaccit nārāyaṇam devam jeṣyāmaḥ samitiṃjayam prāptavān asi sarveṣām asmākam pārthivam padam iti kumbhāṇḍa vacanaiḥ coditaḥ puruṣa ṛṣabhaḥ bāṇaḥ vācam asaṃsaktām provāca vadatām varaḥ cirāt prabhṛti kumbhāṇḍa na yuddham prāpyate mayā tadā mayā mudā pṛṣṭaḥ śitikaṇṭhaḥ pratāpavān yuddha abhilāṣaḥ su mahān deva saṃjāyate mama api prāpsyāmi aham yuddham manasaḥ tuṣṭi kārakam prāpsyase su mahat yuddham tvam bāṇa apratimam mahat iti evam uktaḥ kumbhāṇḍaḥ provāca nṛpatim tadā aho na śobhanam rājan yat evam bhāṣase vacaḥ dhvajaḥ papāta vegena śakra aśani samāhataḥ tam tathā patitam dṛṣṭvā saḥ asuraḥ dhvajam uttamam tatas cakampe vasudhā śakra aśani samāhatā nanāda antarhitaḥ bhūmau vṛṣadaṃśaḥ jagarja ca śoṇitam śoṇitapure sarvatas puram antikāt sva pakṣe coditaḥ sūryaḥ bharaṇīm samapīḍayat caitya vṛkṣeṣu sahasā dhārāḥ śata sahasraśas śoṇitasya asravan ghorāḥ nipetuḥ tārakāḥ bhṛśam loka kṣaya kare kāle nirghātaḥ ca abhavat mahān dakṣiṇām diśam āsthāya dhūmaketuḥ sthitaḥ abhavat aniśam ca api avicchinnāḥ vavuḥ vātāḥ su dāruṇāḥ tri varṇaḥ parighaḥ bhānoḥ saṃdhyā rāgam atha āvṛṇot vakram aṅgārakaḥ cakre kṛttikāsu bhayaṃkaraḥ aneka śākhaḥ caityaḥ ca nipapāta mahī tale arcitaḥ sarva kanyābhiḥ dānavānām mahātmanām evam vividha rūpāṇi nimittāni niśāmayan vicetāḥ tu abhavat prājñaḥ kumbhāṇḍaḥ tattva darśivān bāṇaḥ prīta manāḥ tu eva papau pānam anuttamam kumbhāṇḍaḥ cintayā āviṣṭaḥ rāja veśma abhyayāt tadā rājā pramādī durbuddhiḥ jita kāśī mahā asuraḥ yuddham eva abhilaṣate na doṣān paśyate madāt api idānīm bhavet mithyā sarvam utpāta darśanam iha tu āste trinayanaḥ kārttikeyaḥ ca vīryavān utpanna doṣa prabhavaḥ kṣayaḥ ayam bhavitā mahān doṣāṇām na bhavet nāśaḥ iti me dhīyate matiḥ daurātmyāt nṛpateḥ asya doṣa bhūtāḥ hi dānavāḥ deva dānava saṃghānām yaḥ kartā bhuvana prabhuḥ sa bhavaḥ kārttikeyaḥ ca kṛtavāsaḥ hi naḥ pure tat viśiṣṭaḥ ca bāṇaḥ api bhavasya satatam priyaḥ śaktaḥ dātum samāgamya bāṇa sāhāyya kāṅkṣiṇoḥ evam sa cintayā āviṣṭaḥ kumbhāṇḍaḥ tattva darśivān svasti praṇihitām buddhim cakāra sa mahā asuraḥ vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca krīḍā vihāra upagataḥ kadācid abhavat bhavaḥ devyā saha nadī tīre ramye śrīmati sa prabhuḥ śatāni tatra apsarasām cikrīḍuḥ ca samantataḥ kusumaiḥ pārijātasya puṣpaiḥ saṃtānakasya ca gandha uddāmam iva ākāśam nadī tīram ca sarvaśas vādyamānaiḥ sa śuśrāva gītam apsarasām tadā sūta māgadha kalpaiḥ ca api astuvan apsaraḥ gaṇāḥ devadevam su vapuṣam sragviṇam rakta vāsasam tatas tu devyāḥ rūpeṇa vikralekhā vara apsarāḥ bhavam prasādayām āsa devī ca prāhasat tadā devī prahāsam mumuce tāḥ ca eva apsarasaḥ tadā tatas kilikilā śabdaḥ prādurbhūtaḥ samantataḥ bāṇasya duhitā kanyā tatra uṣā nāma bhāminī devam prakrīḍitam dṛṣṭvā devyā saha nadī gatam uṣāḥ manoratham cakre pārvatyāḥ saṃnidhau tadā dhanyāḥ hi bhartṛ sahitāḥ ramyanti evam samāgatāḥ vijñāya tam abhiprāyam uṣāyāḥ parvata ātmajā prāha devī tatas vākyam uṣām harṣayatī śanais uṣe śīghram tvam api evam bhartrā saha ramiṣyasi yathā devaḥ mayā sārdham śaṃkaraḥ śatru sūdanaḥ evam ukte tadā devyāḥ vākye cintā āvila īkṣaṇā uṣāḥ bhāvam hṛdā cakre bhartrā raṃsye kadā saha uṣe śṛṇuṣva kalyāṇi yadā saṃyogam eṣyasi vaiśākhe māsi harmya sthām dvādaśyām tvām dinakṣaye evam uktā daitya sutā kanyā gaṇa samāvṛtā apākrāmata harṣeṇa ramamāṇā yathāsukham tatas krīḍā vihāram tam anubhūya saha umayā gate ahani punar sarvāḥ tāḥ nāryaḥ jagmuḥ adbhutāḥ kāḥ cit aśvaiḥ tathā yānaiḥ gajaiḥ anyāḥ tathā rathaiḥ puram praviviśuḥ hṛṣṭāḥ kāścid ākāśam āsthitāḥ tatas harmye śayānām tu vaiśākhe māsi bhāminīm dvādaśyām śukla pakṣasya sakhī gaṇa vṛtām tadā yathā uktaḥ puruṣaḥ svapne dharṣayām āsa tām uṣām sā svapne dharṣitā tena strī bhāvam ca api lambhitā śoṇita aktā prarudatī sahasā eva utthitā niśi tām tathā rudatīm dṛṣṭvā sakhī bhaya samanvitām uṣe mā bhaiḥ kim evam tvam rudatī paritapyase baleḥ suta sutā ca tvam prakhyātā kim bhaya anvitā asakṛt deva sahitaḥ śacī bhartā sureśvaraḥ uṣā uvāca evam saṃdūṣitā sādhvī katham jīvitum utsahe pitaram kim nu vakṣyāmi deva śatrum ariṃdamam evam saṃdūṣaṇa karī vaṃśasya asya mahā ojasaḥ katham evam kṛtā nāma kanyā jīvitum utsahe kula upakrośana karī kula aṅgārā nirāśreyā jīvitum spṛhayet nārī sādhvīnām agratas sthitā iti evam bāṣpa pūrṇa akṣī sakhī jana vṛtā tadā vilalāpa ciram kālam uṣā kamala locanā anāthavat tām rudatīm sakhyaḥ sarvāḥ vicetasaḥ ūcuḥ aśru parīta akṣyaḥ uṣām sarvāḥ samāgatāḥ duṣṭena manasā devi śubham yadi vā asubham duṣṭā yā tribhiḥ etaiḥ tu pāpā sā procyate bhuvi katham tvam doṣa duṣṭā vai niyatā brahmacāriṇī yadi suptā satī sādhvī śuddha bhāvā manasvinī yasyāḥ duṣṭam manaḥ pūrvam karmaṇā ca upapāditam tām āhuḥ asatīm nāma satī tvam asi bhāmini iti evam uktām rudatīm bāṣpeṇa āvṛta locanām kumbhāṇḍa duhitā vākyam paramam tu idam abravīt uṣe yat uktā devyā asi bhartāram dhyāyatī tadā dvādaśyām śukla pakṣasya vaiśākhe māsi yaḥ niśi harmye śayānām rudatīm strī bhāvam upaneṣyati bhavitā sa hi te bhartā śūraḥ śatru nibarhaṇaḥ iti uvāca tatas hṛṣṭā devī tava manogatam na hi tat vacanam mithyā pārvatyāḥ yat udāhṛtam iti evam uktā vacanam smṛtvā devī vacaḥ tatas abhavat naṣṭa śokā sā bāṇa putrī śubha īkṣaṇā uṣā uvāca smarāmi bhāmini vacaḥ devyāḥ krīḍā gate bhave vyādiṣṭaḥ sa katham jñeyaḥ tatra kāryam vidhīyatām vyājahāra yathānyāyam artha tattva viśāradā a dṛṣṭaḥ ca a śrutaḥ ca eva dṛṣṭaḥ svapne tvayā anaghe yena tvam asita apāṅge mattakāśini vikramāt rudatī prasabham bhuktā praviśya antaḥpuram sakhi na hi ayam prākṛtaḥ kaścid yaḥ praviṣṭaḥ prasahya naḥ nagaram loka vikhyātam ekaḥ śatru nibarhaṇaḥ na śaktāḥ śoṇitapuram praveṣṭum bhīma vikramāḥ saḥ ayam etaiḥ śataguṇaḥ viśiṣṭaḥ śatru sūdanaḥ praviṣṭaḥ śoṇita puram bāṇam ākramya mūrdhani kaḥ tasyāḥ jīvitena arthaḥ bhogaiḥ vā syāt śubha īkṣaṇe prāptaḥ devyāḥ prasādena pārvatyāḥ mattakāśini idam tu te kāryatamam śṛṇu tvam vai mayā īritam uvāca kumbhāṇḍa sutām katham jñāsyāmahe sakhi tatas sā cintayā āviṣṭā vacanam prāha durvacaḥ citralekhām apsarasam praṇayāt tām sakhīm sakhī paramam śṛṇu me vākyam yat tvām vakṣyāmi bhāmini tyakṣyāmi aham priyān prāṇān tatas kamala locane vikralekhā bravīt vākyam uṣām tām hasatī śanais kim tu śakyam idam kāryam buddhi pūrvam mayā sakhi prāptum tat śṛṇu me vākyam yathā kāmam avāpsyasi ye viśiṣṭāḥ prabhāvena rūpeṇa abhijanena ca yathā pradhānān tān sarvān ālikhiṣyāmi aham sakhi manuṣya loke ye ca api pravarāḥ loka viśrutāḥ sapta rātreṇa te bhīru darśayiṣyāmi tān aham tatas vijñāya paṭṭa stham bhartāram pratilapsyase iti uktvā sapta rātreṇa kṛtvā ālekhya gatān tu tān citra paṭṭa gatān mukhyān ānayām āsa śobhanā tatas prāstīrya paṭṭam sā citralekhā svayaṃkṛtam uṣāyāḥ darśayām āsa sakhīnām ca viśeṣataḥ ete deveṣu ye mukhyāḥ tathā dānava vaṃśa jāḥ manuṣyāṇām ca sarveṣām ye viśiṣṭatamāḥ narāḥ tat etān paśya sarvān tvam yathā abhilikhitān mayā yaḥ te bhartā yathārūpaḥ yathā abhilaṣitaḥ sakhi tam tvam pratyabhijānīhi svapne yam dṛṣṭavatī asi tatas krameṇa sarvān tān dṛṣṭvā sā mattakāśinī abravīt citralekhām tām ayam coraḥ sa vai sakhi yena aham dūṣitā pūrvam svapne harmya gatā satī saḥ ayam vijñāta rūpaḥ me kutastyaḥ taskaraḥ śubhe guṇa śīla abhijanataḥ nāma kim ca asya bhāmini ayam trilokya nāthasya naptā kṛṣṇasya dhīmataḥ bhartā tava viśāla akṣi prādyumniḥ bhīma vikramaḥ utpāṭya parvatān eṣa parvataiḥ eva śātayet tryakṣa patnyā samādiṣṭaḥ sadṛśaḥ sat janaḥ patiḥ uṣā uvāca yadi enam me viśāla akṣi bhartāram amara upamam adya nā ānayasi kṣipram prāṇān tyakṣyāmi aham śubhe yathā bāṇasya nagaram rakṣyate devi sarvatas dvārakā api tathā bhīru durādharṣā viśeṣataḥ na ca śakyam a vijñātaiḥ praveṣṭum dvārakām purīm ātmānam mām ca rakṣasva pitaram ca viśeṣataḥ uṣā uvāca yadi tvam me vijānāsi sakhyam premṇā ca bhāṣitam kṣipram ānaya me kāntam prāṇān tyakṣyāmi vā priyān eṣā gacchāmi aham bhīru praviśya dvārakām purīm bhartāram ānayāmi adya tava vṛṣṇi kula udvaham sā vacaḥ tathyam aśivam dānavānām bhaya āvaham uktvā ca antarhitā kṣipram citralekhā manojavā tṛtīye tu muhūrte sā naṣṭā bāṇapurāt tadā kṣaṇena samanuprāptāḥ dvārakām kṛṣṇa pālitām dadarśa dvārakām ca eva divi tārā iva saṃsthitām vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca dadarśa madhye nārīṇām tārāpatim iva uditam cintayā āviṣṭa dehā sā citralekhā manasvinī sā antarhitā cintayitvā citralekhā yaśasvinī tāmasyā chādayām āsa vidyayā śubha locanā harmye strī gaṇa madhya stham kṛtvā ca antarhitam tadā utpapāta gṛhītvā sā prādyumnim yuddha durmadam sahasā śoṇitapuram praviveśa manaḥ javā tatra uṣāḥ vismitā dṛṣṭvā harmya sthā sakhi saṃnidhau praveśayām āsa tadā saṃprāptam sva gṛham tatas praharṣa utphulla nayanā priyam dṛṣṭvā artha kovidā tvaritā kāminī prāha citralekhām bhaya āturā sakhi idam vai katham guhyam kāryam kārya viśārade guhye kṛte bhavet svasti prakāśe jīvita kṣayaḥ iti uktvā tvaramāṇā sā guhya deśe su alaṃkṛtā ramamāṇā aniruddhena avijñātā tu sā tadā tasmin eva kṣaṇe prāpte yadūnām ṛṣabhaḥ hi saḥ uṣayā saha saṃyuktaḥ vijñātaḥ bāṇa rakṣibhiḥ tatas taiḥ cāra puruṣaiḥ bāṇasya āveditam tadā yathādṛṣṭam aśeṣeṇa kanyāyāḥ tad vyatikramam tatas kiṃkara sainyam tu vyādiṣṭam bhīma karmaṇā baleḥ putreṇa bāṇena vīreṇa amitra ghātinā uṣāyām dharṣitāyām hi kulam naḥ dharṣitam mahat asaṃpradānāt yaḥ asmābhiḥ svayaṃgrāham adharṣayat yaḥ puram bhavanam ca idam praviṣṭaḥ naḥ su bāliśaḥ nānā śastra udyata karāḥ nānā rūpa dharāḥ tu te dānavāḥ samabhikruddhāḥ prādyumni vadha kāṅkṣiṇaḥ tatas teṣām svanam śrutvā sarveṣām eva garjatām tam āpatantam dṛṣṭvā eva saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭham mahā bhujam prāsādāt ca avarohantam bhaya ārtāḥ vipradudruvuḥ vadhāya teṣām cikṣepa nānā āyudha viśāradaḥ asibhiḥ śaktibhiḥ śūlaiḥ nijaghnuḥ raṇa gocaram sa hanyamānaḥ nārācaiḥ parighaiḥ ca samantataḥ na akṣubhyat sarva bhūta ātmā nadan meghaḥ iva uṣṇage āvidhya parigham ghoram teṣām madhye vyavasthitaḥ aniruddhaḥ nadan hṛṣṭaḥ siṃhanādam vyarocata yatas bāṇaḥ tatas bhītāḥ yayuḥ yuddha parāṅmukhāḥ tatas bāṇa samīpa sthāḥ śvasantaḥ rudhira ukṣitāḥ mā bhaiṣṭa mā bhaiṣṭa iti rājñā te tena coditāḥ trāsam visṛjya ca ekasthāḥ yudhyadhvam dānava ṛṣabhāḥ kim idam loka vikhyātam yaśaḥ utsṛjya dūratas vyādideśa raṇe śūrān sarvān ayutaśas punar pramātha gaṇa bhūyiṣṭham vyādiṣṭam tasya nigrahe anīkam su mahat rātrau nānā praharaṇa udyatam bāṇa anīkaiḥ samabhavat vyāptam saṃdīpta locanaiḥ kecid kṣiti sthāḥ prākrośan gajāḥ iva samantataḥ tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti ca tadā vācaḥ aśrūyanta sarvaśas teṣām eva ca jagrāha parighān tomarān api nistriṃśam carma ca utsṛṣṭam jagrāha raṇa mūrdhani sa tena vyacarat mārgān ekaḥ śatru nibarhaṇaḥ iti prakārān dvātriṃśat vicaran abhyadṛśyata ekam sahasraśas tatra dadṛśuḥ raṇa mūrdhani punar bhagnāḥ prādravanta yatra bāṇaḥ sthitaḥ raṇe kṛtvā ca ārta svaram ghoram diśaḥ jagmuḥ hata ojasaḥ ekaikasya upari tadā te anyonyam bhaya pīḍitāḥ vamantaḥ śoṇitam jagmuḥ viṣādāt vimukhāḥ raṇe na babhūva bhayam tādṛś gānavānām purā raṇe yādṛśam yudhyamānānām aniruddhena saṃyuge dānavāḥ giri śṛṅga ābhāḥ gadā śūla asi pāṇayaḥ te bāṇam utsṛjya raṇe jagmuḥ bhaya samākulāḥ viśālam ākāśa talam dānavāḥ nirjitāḥ tadā niḥsaṅga bhagnām mahatīm dṛṣṭvā tām vāhinīm tadā kumbhāṇḍa saṃgṛhītam tu ratham āsthāya vīryavān paṭṭisa asi gadā śūlān udyamya ca paraśvadhān baddha godhā aṅgulitraiḥ ca bāhubhiḥ sa mahā bhujaḥ nānā praharaṇa upetaḥ śuśubhe dānava uttamaḥ abravīt tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti krodha saṃrakta locanaḥ āpatantam dadarśa atha khaḍga carma dharam tadā khaḍga carma dharam tam tu dṛṣṭvā bāṇaḥ padātinam praharṣam atulam lebhe prādyumni vadha kāṅkṣayā tanutreṇa vihīnaḥ ca khaḍga pāṇiḥ ca yādavaḥ vavarṣa śara jālāni kṣudrakāṇām samantataḥ bāṇaḥ aniruddha śirasi kāṅkṣan tasya raṇe vadham tāni bāṇa sahasrāṇi carmaṇā vyavadhūya saḥ saḥ abhibhūya raṇe bāṇam āsthitaḥ yadu nandanaḥ siṃhaḥ pramukhatas dṛṣṭvā gajam ekam yathā vane rudhira ogha plutaiḥ gātraiḥ bāṇa varṣaiḥ samāhataḥ jaghāna aśvān ca khaḍgena bāṇasya ratha mūrdhani tam punar śara varṣeṇa tomaraiḥ paṭṭisaiḥ tathā hataḥ ayam iti vijñāya prāṇadat nairṛtāḥ gaṇāḥ śaktim bāṇaḥ tatas kruddhaḥ ghora rūpām bhaya āvahām jagrāha jvalitām dīptām ghaṇṭā mālā ākulām raṇe jvalana āditya saṃkāśām yama daṇḍa upamām śubhām prāhiṇot tām asaṅgena mahā ulkām jvalitām iva saḥ abhiplutya tadā śaktim jagrāha puruṣottamaḥ nirbibheda tatas bāṇam sva śaktyā eva mahā balaḥ tatas mūrchā abhibhūtam tam kumbhāṇḍaḥ vākyam abravīt māyām āśritya yudhyasva na ayam vadhyaḥ anyathā bhavet ātmānam mām ca rakṣasva pramādāt kim upekṣase kumbhāṇḍa vacanaiḥ evam dānava indraḥ pracoditaḥ vācam rūkṣām abhikruddhaḥ provāca vadatām varaḥ eṣaḥ aham asya vidadhe mṛtyum prāṇa haram raṇe iti evam uktvā sa rathaḥ sa aśvaḥ sa dhvaja sārathiḥ vijñāya antarhitam bāṇam prādyumniḥ aparājitaḥ pauruṣeṇa samāyuktaḥ sa praikṣata diśaḥ daśa āsthāya tāmasīm vidyām tadā kruddhaḥ baleḥ sutaḥ veṣṭitaḥ bahudhā tasya dehaḥ pannaga rāśibhiḥ sa tu veṣṭita sarva aṅgaḥ baddhaḥ prādyumniḥ āhave jvālā avalīḍha vadanaiḥ sarpa bhogaiḥ viveṣṭitaḥ abhītaḥ parvata ākāraḥ prādyumniḥ abhavat raṇe niṣprayatnaḥ sthitaḥ ca api sarpa bhoga mayaiḥ śaraiḥ bāṇaḥ dhvajam samāśritya provāca amarṣitaḥ vacaḥ kumbhāṇḍa vadhyatām śīghram ayam vai kula pāṃsanaḥ cāritram yena me loke dūṣitam dūṣita ātmanā rājan vakṣyāmi aham kiṃcid tat me śṛṇu yadi icchasi ayam vijñāyatām kasya kutas vā ayam iha āgataḥ mayā ayam bahuśas yuddhe dṛṣṭaḥ rājan mahā raṇe krīḍan iva ca yuddheṣu dṛśyate deva sūnu vat vijñāya ca vadham vā asya pūjām vā api kariṣyasi vadhe hi asya mahān doṣaḥ rakṣaṇe su mahān guṇaḥ ayam hi puruṣa utkarṣaḥ sarvathā mānam arhati paśya rājan yaśaḥ vīryaiḥ anvitam puruṣottamam na naḥ gaṇayate sarvān vadham prāptaḥ api ayam balī śoṇita ogha plutaiḥ gātraiḥ nāgabhogaiḥ ca veṣṭitaḥ imām avasthām nītaḥ api sva bāhu balam āśritaḥ na cintayati rājan tvām vīryavān kaḥ api asau yuvā rucitam yadi te rājan jñeyaḥ vīrya bala anvitaḥ kumbhāṇḍasya vacaḥ śrutvā śuddhānte pannagaiḥ bhṛśam aniruddham yodhayitvā bāṇaḥ sva gṛham āviśat vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca priyam nātham a paśyantyaḥ kiṃnaryaḥ iva saṃghaśas anāthāḥ iva saṃtrastāḥ rudimaḥ bhaya mohitāḥ bāhu chāyām samāśritya vasanti divi devatāḥ yasya aniruddhaḥ pautraḥ sa vīraḥ kena api naḥ hṛtaḥ vyādita āsyasya yaḥ mṛtyoḥ daṃṣṭra agre parivartate idam evaṃvidham kṛtvā vipriyam yadu puṃgave kṛta āgāḥ na sa śocyaḥ tu vayam tu adya vinākṛtāḥ viprayogena nāthasya kṛtānta vaśagāḥ kṛtāḥ netra jam vāri mumucuḥ aśivam parama aṅganāḥ tāsām bāṣpa abhipūrṇāni nayanāni cakāśire salilena āplutāni iva paṅkajāni jalāgame tāsām arāla pakṣmāṇi rājimanti śubhāni ca rudhireṇa āplutāni iva nayanāni cakāśire kurarīṇām iva ākāśe rudatīnām sahasraśas utpetuḥ sahasā khebhyaḥ gṛhebhyaḥ puruṣa ṛṣabhāḥ kasmāt eṣaḥ aniruddhasya śrūyate su mahā svanaḥ gṛhe kṛṣṇa abhiguptānām kutas naḥ bhayam āgatam pradharṣitāḥ yathā siṃhāḥ guhābhyaḥ iva niḥsṛtāḥ saṃnāha bherī kṛṣṇasya sabhāyām āhatā tadā tasyāḥ śabdena te sarve sabhām āgamya viṣṭhitāḥ kim etat iti ca anyonyam samapṛcchanta yādavāḥ niḥśvasantaḥ vyatiṣṭhanta yādavāḥ yuddha durmadāḥ tūṣṇīṃbhūteṣu sarveṣu vipṛthuḥ vākyam abravīt kim evam cintayā āviṣṭaḥ puruṣa indra bhavān iha tava bāhu bala prāṇam āśritāḥ sarva yādavāḥ bhavantam āśritāḥ kṛṣṇa saṃvibhaktāḥ ca sarvaśas sukham svapiti niścintaḥ katham tvam cintayā anvitaḥ śoka sāgaram akṣobhyam sarve te jñātayaḥ gatāḥ kim evam cintayā āviṣṭaḥ na kiṃcid api bhāṣase cintām kartum vṛthā deva na tvam arhasi mādhava iti evam uktaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ tu niḥśvasya su ciram punar prāha vākyam sa vākya jñaḥ bṛhaspatiḥ iva svayam vipṛtho cintayā āviṣṭaḥ etat kāryam vicintayan dāśārha gaṇa madhye adya vadāmi arthavatīm giram śṛṇudhvam yādavāḥ sarve yathā cintā anvitaḥ hi aham aśaktān iva manyante sarvān asmān sa bāndhavān āhukaḥ ca eva naḥ rājā hṛtaḥ śālvena vai purā pratyānītaḥ tatas asmābhiḥ yuddham kṛtvā su dāruṇam pradyumnaḥ ca api naḥ bālye śambareṇa hṛtaḥ hi abhūt idam tu su mahat kaṣṭam prādyumniḥ kva pravāsitaḥ na evaṃvidham aham doṣam smarāmi manuja ṛṣabhāḥ bhasmanā guṇṭhitaḥ pādaḥ yena me mūrdhni pātitaḥ sa anubandhasya tasya aham hariṣye jīvitam raṇe iti evam ukte kṛṣṇena sātyakiḥ vākyam abravīt carāḥ kṛṣṇa prayujyantām aniruddhasya mārgaṇe āhukam prāha kṛṣṇaḥ tu smitam kṛtvā vacaḥ tadā ābhyantarāḥ ca bāhyāḥ ca vyādiśyantām carāḥ nṛpa tatas carāḥ tu vyādiṣṭāḥ pārthivena yaśasvinā veṇumantam latāveṣṭam tathā raivatakam girim ṛkṣavantam girim ca eva mārgadhvam tvaritāḥ hayaiḥ gantavyam ca api niḥsaṅgam udyāneṣu samantataḥ hayānām ca sahasrāṇi rathānām ca api anekaśas śṛṇu kṛṣṇa vacaḥ mahyam yadi te rocate vibho hayagrīvaḥ ca su mahān sa anubandhaḥ tvayā hataḥ tādṛśe vigrahe vṛtte deva hetoḥ su dāruṇe sarvāṇi etāni karmāṇi niḥśeṣāṇi raṇe raṇe kṛtavān asi govinda pārṣṇigrāhaḥ ca na asti te idam karma tvayā kṛṣṇa sa anubandham kṛtam raṇe pārijātasya haraṇe yat kṛtam karma duṣkaram tatra śakraḥ tvayā kṛṣṇaḥ airāvata śiraḥ gataḥ nirjitaḥ bāhu vīryeṇa svayam yuddha viśāradaḥ tatra aniruddha haraṇam kṛtam maghavatā svayam uvāca vacanam dhīmān anādhṛṣṭim mahā balam senā nīḥ tāta mā mā evam na devāḥ kṣudra karmiṇaḥ na akṛtajñāḥ na vā klībāḥ na avaliptāḥ na bāliśāḥ devatā artham ca me yatnaḥ mahān dānava saṃkṣaye teṣām priya artham ca raṇe hanmi dṛptān mahā asurān katham pāpam kariṣyanti vijñāya evaṃvidham hi mām akṣudrāḥ satyavantaḥ ca nityam bhakta anuyāyinaḥ na teṣu pāpam vijñeyam bāliśa tvam prabhāṣase asmākam ca api yat kāryam tat ca kāryam śacīpateḥ saṃrakṣyāḥ ca vayam devaiḥ asmābhiḥ ca api devatāḥ snigdha gambhīrayā vācā punar kṛṣṇaḥ abhyabhāṣata pradyumna putraḥ apahṛtaḥ puṃścalyā tu hṛtaḥ striyā māyā vidagdhāḥ puṃścalyaḥ daitya dānava yoṣitaḥ madhuraḥ śrūyate śabdaḥ mādhavasya niveśane te carāḥ sarvatas sarve sabhā dvāram upāgatāḥ udyānāni śilāḥ śailāḥ guhāḥ nadyaḥ sarāṃsi ca ekaikam śataśas rājan mārgitam na ca dṛśyeta tat ājñāpaya naḥ kṣipram aniruddhasya mārgaṇe saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭāḥ kecid kecid bāṣpa āvila īkṣaṇāḥ kecid ca acintan śūrāḥ āsthāya bhrukuṭim narāḥ prabodhanam mahā bāhoḥ kṛṣṇasya akriyata ālaye atha abhyutthāya vimanāḥ kṛṣṇaḥ samiti durjayaḥ sa viṣṭare sthitaḥ śubhre spardhya āstaraṇa saṃvṛte ṛju āsīnaḥ yathānyāyam uvāca idam vacaḥ arthavat kim evam cintayā āviṣṭāḥ niḥsaṅgam tamasā āvṛtāḥ utsāha nāśāt sarve vai klībāḥ iva vicetasaḥ vāsudevaḥ abravīt vākyam śrūyatām bhagavan idam tasya arthe sarve eva āsma cintayā āviṣṭa cetasaḥ uṣāḥ nāma sutā tasya bāṇasya apratima ojasaḥ tasya arthe citralekhā vai jahāra āśu tam apsarāḥ prādyumni bāṇayoḥ saṃkhye bali vāsavayoḥ iva baṇena māyām āsthāya baddhaḥ nāgaiḥ mahā balaḥ uttiṣṭhatu bhavān śīghram yaśase vijayāya ca prāyātrikān vai saṃbhārān ājñāpayata vīryavān tatas candana cūrṇaiḥ ca lājaiḥ ca api samantataḥ nāradaḥ uvāca tat itas śoṇitapuram prādyumniḥ yatra sāṃpratam ekena sa muhūrtena bāṇam saṃdarśayiṣyati sa kṛṣṇa pārśvam āgamya harṣeṇa avasthitaḥ abhavat kṛṣṇa keśaḥ pravalayaḥ jiṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ ca varṇataḥ catur daṃṣṭraḥ catur bāhuḥ catur veda ṣaḍaṅga vid śrīvatsa aṅkaḥ aravinda akṣaḥ ūrdhva lomāḥ mṛdu tvacaḥ sama aṅguliḥ sama nakhaḥ rakta aṅguli nakha antaraḥ ājānu bāhuḥ siṃha āsyaḥ siṃha saṃhananaḥ yuvā yaḥ prabhuḥ bhāti bhūtātmā bhūtānām bhāvanaḥ prabhuḥ yasya aṣṭaguṇam aiśvaryam dadau prītaḥ prajāpatiḥ prajāpatīnām sādhyānām tridaśānām ca śāśvataḥ saṃvidhānam atha ājñāpya dvārakāyām mahā bhujaḥ gamanāya matim cakre vāsudevaḥ pratāpavān āsthitaḥ garuḍam devaḥ tasya ca anu halāyudhaḥ pṛṣṭhatas anu balasya api pradyumnaḥ śatru karśanaḥ jaya bāṇam mahā bāho ye ca asya anucarāḥ raṇe prasāde te dhruvā lakṣmīḥ vijayaḥ ca parākrame śṛṇvan vācaḥ antarikṣa sthaḥ prayayau keśavaḥ raṇe vaiśampāyanaḥ uvāca bandi māgadha sūtānām sarvaiḥ ca api sahasraśas sa tu unmukhaiḥ jaya āśīrbhiḥ stūyamānaḥ hi mānavaiḥ babhāra rūpam soma arka śakrāṇām sadṛśam hariḥ atīva śuśubhe rūpam tasya ca abhyutpatiṣyataḥ atha aṣṭa bāhuḥ kṛṣṇaḥ tu parvata ākāra saṃnibhaḥ vibabhau puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ vikāṅkṣan bāṇa saṃkṣayam carma śārṅgam tathā cāpam śaṅkham ca eva asya vāmatas śīrṣāṇām vai sahasram tu vihitam śārṅgadhanvanā sahasram ca eva kāyānām vahan saṃkarṣaṇaḥ tadā śveta praharaṇaḥ adhṛṣyaḥ kailāsaḥ iva śṛṅgavān āsthitaḥ garuḍam rāmaḥ udyan iva niśākaraḥ pradyumnasya mahā bāhoḥ saṃgrāme vikramiṣyataḥ jagāma mārgam balavān vātasya pratiṣedhayan atha rāmaḥ abravīt vākyam kṛṣṇam apratimam raṇe svābhiḥ prabhābhiḥ hīnāḥ smaḥ kṛṣṇa kasmāt apūrvavat sarve kanaka varṇa ābhāḥ saṃvṛttāḥ sma na saṃśayaḥ kim idam brūhi naḥ tattvam kim meroḥ pārśva gāḥ vayam agneḥ āhavanīyasya prabhayā sma samāhatāḥ tena naḥ varṇa vairūpyam idam jātam halāyudha rāmaḥ uvāca yadi sma saṃnikarṣa sthāḥ yadi niṣprabha tām gatāḥ bhagavān uvāca agniḥ āhavanīyaḥ tu tatas śāntim upāgamat kṛṣṇaḥ saṃkarṣaṇaḥ ca eva pradyumnaḥ ca mahā balaḥ tatas praśānte dahane saṃpratasthe sa pakṣirāj tān dṛṣṭvā acintayan tatra rudrasya anucara agnayaḥ āsthitāḥ garuḍam hi ete nānā rūpāḥ bhaya āvāhāḥ kim artham iha saṃprāptāḥ ke vā api ime janāḥ trayaḥ prāvartayan ca saṃgrāmam taiḥ tribhiḥ saha yādavaiḥ atha ājñaptaḥ tu bāṇena puruṣaḥ vai manojavaḥ agnīnām vāsudevena saṃsaktānām mahā mṛdhe te jātavedasaḥ sarve kalmāṣaḥ khasṛmaḥ tathā svadhākārasya viṣaye prakhyātāḥ pañca vahnayaḥ atha apare mahābhāgāḥ svaiḥ anīkaiḥ vyavasthitāḥ paṭaraḥ patagaḥ svarṇaḥ agādhaḥ bhrājaḥ eva ca svāhākāra āśrayā pañca ayudhyan te api ca agnayaḥ jyotiṣṭoma haviḥ bhāgau vaṣaṭkāra āśrayau punar dvau agnī saṃprayudhyetām mahātmānau mahā dyutī sthitam aṅgirasam dṛṣṭvā syandane puruṣottamaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ provāca vacanam smayan iva punar punar mama astra tejasā dadghāḥ diśaḥ yāsyatha vikṣatāḥ atha aṅgirāḥ triśūlena dīptena samadhāvata ādadānaḥ iva krodhāt kṛṣṇa prāṇān madhāmṛdhe triśūlam tasya tat dīptam ciccheda parama iṣubhiḥ sthūṇākarṇena ca astreṇa dīptena su mahā yaśāḥ tatas śaṅkham samānīya vadane puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ tatas pradhmāpya tam śaṅkham bhayam utpādya vīryavān bāṇa anīkāni sahasā samanahyanta sarvaśas koṭiśas ca api bahudhā dīpta praharaṇam tadā pramātha gaṇa mukhyāḥ ca te ayudhyan kṛṣṇa saṃgatāḥ sarvatas taiḥ pradīpta āsyaiḥ sa arciṣmadbhiḥ iva analaiḥ āpīyata tadā raktam caturṇām api saṃyuge iti saṃcoditaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ balabhadreṇa dhīmatā teṣām vadha artham āgneyam jagrāha puruṣottamaḥ sa vidhūya asura gaṇān kravyādān astra tejasā śūla paṭṭi sa śakti ṛṣṭi pināka parigha āyudham śaila megha pratīkāśaiḥ nānā rūpaiḥ bhayānakaiḥ vāhanaiḥ saṃghaśas sarve yodhāḥ tatra avatasthire vainateya gataḥ rāmaḥ tān dṛṣṭvā kṛṣṇam abravīt yudhyataḥ prāc mukhasya astu suparṇaḥ vai mama agratas giri śṛṅga nibhaiḥ yuddhe gadā musala lāṅgalaiḥ dānavān puruṣa vyāghraḥ yudyamānān sahasraśas pradhmāpya bahuśas śaṅkham ayudhyata janārdanaḥ pakṣa prahāra abhihatān tuṇḍa agra nakha vikṣatān taiḥ hanyamānam daityānām anīkam bhīma vikramaiḥ abhajyata tadā saṃkhye bāṇa varṣa samāhatam bhajyamāneṣu anīkeṣu trātu kāmaḥ samabhyayāt rauhiṇeyasya śaighryeṇa na avasthānam adṛśyata pradīptam patitam tat tu giri śṛṅgam vyadārayat roṣeṇa abhiprajajvāla bhasmanā kṛṣṇapūrvajaḥ niḥśvasan jṛmbhamāṇaḥ ca nidrā anvita tanuḥ muhur saṃhṛṣṭa lomā glāna akṣaḥ kṣipta cittaḥ iva śvasan tatas haladharaḥ mattaḥ kṛṣṇam āha vicetanaḥ kṛṣṇena parama snehāt tatas dāhāt pramucyata cikṣepa ca enam tat bhasma jvālā garbham mahā gadaḥ jaghāna kṛṣṇam grīvāyām muṣṭinā ekena ca urasi yam akṣayam samupanayan jagatpatiḥ śarīradhṛk gaganacaram mahā mṛdhe vaiśampāyanaḥ uvāca mṛtaḥ iti abhivijñāya jvaram śatru niṣūdanaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ bhuja balābhyām tam cikṣepa atha mahī tale mukta mātraḥ tu bāhubhyām kṛṣṇa deham viveśa saḥ a muktvā vigraham tasya kṛṣṇasya apratima ojasaḥ sa hi āviṣṭaḥ tadā tena jvareṇa apratima ojasā jṛmbhate ca tadā kṛṣṇaḥ punar ca skhalate bhṛśam ca utthita gātraḥ tu nidrayā ca abhibhūyate jvara abhimṛṣṭam ātmānam vijñāya puruṣottamaḥ aśarīrā tatas vāṇī tam uvāca antarikṣa gā mā vadhīḥ jvaram etam vai rakṣaṇīyaḥ tvayā anagha tava prasādāt nā anyaḥ syāt jvaraḥ samiti śobhana bhagavān uvāca yaḥ ayam mayā jvaraḥ sṛṣṭaḥ mayi eva eṣa pralīyatām ājñāpaya kim te cakrāyudha karomi aham bhagavān uvāca evam uktaḥ tu kṛṣṇena jvaraḥ sākṣāt mahātmanā praṇamya śirasā kṛṣṇam apakrāntaḥ tatas raṇāt vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca cakra lāṅgala pātaiḥ ca bāṇa varṣaiḥ ca pīḍitam saṃcukopa mahā anīkam dānavānām mahātmanām kakṣe agniḥ iva saṃvṛddhaḥ śuṣka indhana samīritāḥ kṛṣṇa bāṇa agniḥ udbhūtaḥ vivṛddhim upagacchati tām dīryamāṇām mahatīm nānā praharaṇa udyatām senām bāṇaḥ samāsādya vārayan vākyam abravīt lāghavam samupāgamya kim evam bhaya viklavāḥ daitya vaṃśa samutpannāḥ palāyadhyvam mahā mṛdhe kavaca asi gadā prāsān khaḍga carma paraśvadhān evam uccāritām vācam śṛṇvantaḥ tām a cintya vai pramātha gaṇa śeṣam tu tad anīka puraḥsaram bhagna avaśeṣam yuddhāya manaḥ cakre punar tadā kumbhāṇḍaḥ nāma bāṇa stha sakhā amātyaḥ ca vīryavān eṣa bāṇaḥ sthitaḥ yuddhe śaṃkaraḥ ayam guhaḥ tathā nandīśvara samāyuktam ratham āsthāya vīryavān saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭaḥ rudraḥ pradhāvati yatas hariḥ piban iva tad ākāśam siṃha yuktaḥ mahā svanaḥ rathaḥ bhāti ghana unmuktaḥ paurṇamāsyām yathā śaśī harim vivyādha kupitaḥ nārācānām śatena saḥ tatas viśīrṇa dehāḥ te catvāraḥ api samantataḥ na adṛśyanta śaraiḥ channāḥ dahyamānāḥ ca vahninā udyate vāruṇa astre tu vāsudevena dhīmatā āgneyam praśamam yātam astram vāruṇa tejasā paiśācam rākṣasam ca eva raudram āṅgirasam bhavaḥ mumoca astrāṇi catvāri yugānta agni nibhāni saḥ vāyavyam atha sāvitram vāsavam mohanam tathā atrāṇām vāruṇa arthāya vāsudevaḥ mumoca ha astraiḥ caturbhiḥ catvāri vārayitvā ca keśavaḥ jagrāha vaiṣṇavam saḥ astram vyādita āsya antaka upamam vaiṣṇava astre vimukte tu sarve eva asura uttamāḥ tripurānta karam bāṇam jagrāha ca caturmukhaḥ vijñātaḥ vāsudevena citta jñena mahātmanā jṛmbhaṇam nām saḥ api astram jagrāha puruṣottamaḥ haram saṃjṛmbhayām āsa kṣiprakārī mahā balaḥ devam vijṛmbhitam dṛṣṭvā sarva bhūtāni tatrasuḥ kumbha aṇḍa saṃgṛhīta aśve rathe tiṣṭhan guhaḥ tadā abhidudrāva kṛṣṇam ca balam pradyumnam eva ca śara ogha ācita gātrāḥ te trayaḥ trayaḥ iva agnayaḥ astram brahmaśiraḥ nāma kāla kalpam durāsadam saṃdaṣṭa oṣṭha puṭaḥ saṃkhye jagrāha sa guhaḥ tadā mahābhūteṣu sarveṣu pradhāvatsu tatas tatas keśavaḥ keśimathanaḥ cakram jagrāha vīryavān sarveṣām astra vīryāṇām vāraṇe ghātane tathā meghaiḥ iva ātapa apāye savituḥ maṇḍalam mahat tatas niṣprabha tām yāte naṣṭa vīrye mahā ojasi tasmin brahmaśirasi astre śaktim jagrāha kāñcanīm ghaṇṭā mālā ākulām śaktim cikṣepa ruṣitaḥ guhaḥ jṛmbhamāṇā iva gagane saṃpradīpta mukhī tatas āghāvantī mahā śaktiḥ kṛṣṇasya vadha kāṅkṣiṇī bhṛśam viṣaṇṇāḥ sa indrāḥ ca sarva amara purogamāḥ śaktim prajvalitām dṛṣṭvā dagdhaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ iti bruvan abhyāśam āgatām tām tu mahā śaktim mahā mṛdhe huṃkāreṇa eva nirbhartsya pātayām āsa bhū tale pātitāyām mahā śaktyām sādhu sādhu iti sarvaśas punar cakram sa jagrāha bhūta anta karaṇam tadā vyāvidhyamāne cakre tu kṛṣṇena amita tejasā tam dṛṣṭvā pramukhe tasya vyatiṣṭhita ca kauṭavī āyāntam atha tam dṛṣṭvā yadūnām ṛṣabham raṇe bāṇaḥ uvāca dvārakām dvārakā sthān vā suhṛdaḥ drakṣyase na ca suvarṇa varṇān vṛkṣa agrān adya drakṣyasi mādhava mayā abhibhūtaḥ samare muhūrṣuḥ kāla coditaḥ dvārakām śoṇitapure nihataḥ saṃsmariṣyasi adya bāhu sahasram me koṭi bhūtam niśāmaya niścaranti mahā ghorāḥ vāta uddhūtāḥ iva ūrmayaḥ bhagavān uvāca bāṇa kim garjase śūrāḥ na garjanti raṇe sthitāḥ ehi ehi jaya mām bāṇa jitaḥ vā vasudhā tale cirāya avāṅmukhaḥ dīnaḥ patitaḥ śeṣyase asura musalaiḥ paṭṭisaiḥ ca api chādayām āsa keśavam aṣṭa bāhuḥ sahasreṇa bāhūnām samayudhyata brahmaṇā vihitam pūrvam tat mumoca baleḥ sutaḥ tasmin mukte diśaḥ sarvāḥ tama upahata maṇḍalāḥ sādhu sādhu iti bāṇam tu pūjayanti sma dānavāḥ aho dhik iti devānām carate vāc udīritā tatas astra bala vegena sa arciṣmatyaḥ su dāruṇāḥ ghora rūpāḥ mahā vegāḥ niṣpetuḥ bāṇa vṛṣṭayaḥ na akampata tadā vāyuḥ na meghāḥ saṃcaranti ca tatas astram su mahā vegam jagrāha madhusūdanaḥ pārjanyam nāma bhagavān yama antaka nibham raṇe dānava astram praśāntam tu parjanya astre abhimantrite bhūyas saṃchādayām āsa keśavam garuḍe sthitam musalaiḥ paṭṭisaiḥ ca eva śūlam udgara kasmaraiḥ tasya tām tarasā sarvām bāṇa vṛṣṭim samudyatām punar saṃvārayām āsa keśavaḥ śatru sūdanaḥ pakṣa tuṇḍa prahāraiḥ tau caraṇa agra nakhaiḥ tathā dakṣiṇena ca pakṣeṇa nijaghāna mahā balaḥ padbhyām pārśva abhighātān ca dattvā ghorān anekaśas ākṛṣya ca enam tarasā vikṛṣya ca mahā balaḥ niḥsaṃjñam pātayām āsa gaganāt iva parvatam mayūre pātite tasmin garuḍena atha pakṣiṇā bāṇaḥ parama saṃvignaḥ cintayan kāryam ātmanaḥ tam dīna manasam jñātvā raṇe bāṇam su viklavam cintayat bhagavān rudraḥ bāṇa rakṣaṇam āturaḥ tatas nandim mahādevaḥ prāha gambhīrayā girā pramātha gaṇa pārśve hi na hi me sthāsyate manaḥ yāhi prabhavase tāta bāṇam saṃrakṣa gamyatām yatas bāṇaḥ tatas gatvā bāṇam āha śanais idam daitya imam ratham ātiṣṭha śīghram ehi mahā bala ārūḍhaḥ sa tathā iti uktvā mahādevasya tam ratham dīptam brahmaśiraḥ nāma bāṇaḥ kruddhaḥ ati vīryavān loka saṃrakṣaṇa artham hi tat sṛṣṭam padmayoninā tat cakreṇa nihatya astram prāha kṛṣṇaḥ pratāpavān loke prakhyāta yaśasam bāṇam apratimam raṇe kārtavīrya arjunaḥ nāma pūrvam āsīt mahā balaḥ sahasra bāhuḥ rāmeṇa dvi bāhuḥ samare kṛtaḥ tathā tava api darpaḥ ayam bāhūnām vīrya saṃbhavaḥ darpa saṃjananān yāvat nāśayiṣyāmi te bhujān tiṣṭha idānīm na me adya tvam mokṣyase raṇa mūrdhani tatas cakram sahasra aram nadan meghaḥ iva uṣṇage jagrāha puruṣa vyāghraḥ bāṇa bāhu praśāntaye jyotiṣmataḥ pataṃgasya śaśinaḥ ca eva yat tathā tejasā tena saṃyuktam bhāsvatā prajvala bhṛśam cakra udyata karam dṛṣṭvā bhagavantam raṇa ajire pramukhe vāsudevasya digvāsāḥ koṭavī sthitā antarghānam upāgamya tyaktvā sā vāsasī punar paritrāṇāya bāṇasya vijayā adhiṣṭhitā tatas bhūyas sa amarṣa tāmra akṣī vivastrā avasthitā raṇe na arhase deva hantum vai bāṇam apratimam raṇe provāca bāṇam samare bhrāmayan cakram uttamam yudhyatām yudhyatām saṃkhye bhavatām koṭavī sthitā aśaktānām iva raṇe dhik bāṇa tava pauruṣam evam uktvā tatas kṛṣṇaḥ tat cakram parama astra vid nimīlita akṣaḥ vyasṛjat bāṇam prati mahā balaḥ alāta cakra vat tūrṇam bhramamāṇam raṇa ajire viṣṇoḥ astram sunābham vai śaighrya yogāt na dṛśyate abhavat parvata ākāraḥ chinna bāhuḥ mahā asuraḥ cakram bhūyas kṣeptu kāmam bāṇa nāśa artham acyutam anivāryam asaṃhāryam raṇe śatru bhayaṃkaram bāṇasya asya abhayam dattam mayā keśiniṣūdana tat me na syāt mṛṣā vākyam atas tvām kṣamayāmi aham bhagavān uvāca jīvatām deva bāṇaḥ ayam etat cakram nivartitam evam uktvā mahādevam kṛṣṇaḥ tūrṇam garutmatā gate kṛṣṇe tatas nandī bāṇam āha śubham vacaḥ bāṇa bāṇa pranṛtyasva śreyaḥ tava bhaviṣyati jīvita arthī tatas bāṇaḥ pramukhe śaṃkarasya vai prānṛtyata bhaye jāte dānavaḥ sa vicetanaḥ avasthām kṛpaṇām prāptaḥ bhaya viklava locanaḥ bāṇaḥ uvāca anyam varam vṛṇīṣva adya anugrāhyaḥ asi me sadā bāṇaḥ uvāca yathā aham śoṇita ādigdhaḥ bhṛśa ārtaḥ vraṇa pīḍitaḥ mad bhaktāḥ ye hi nṛtyanti teṣām evam bhaviṣyati bāṇaḥ uvāca cakra pāṭana jā ghorā rujā tīvrā hi yā mama vareṇa sā tṛtīyena śāntim gacchatu me bhava akṣatam tava gātram ca svastha avastham bhaviṣyati caturtham te varam dadmi vṛṇīṣva asura kāṅkṣitam bāṇaḥ uvāca pramātha gaṇa vaṃśasya prathamaḥ syām aham vibho mahākālaḥ iti khyātaḥ khyātim gaccheyam īśvara bāṇaḥ uvāca tatas abravīt mahādevaḥ bāṇam sthitam atha antike vaiśaṃpāyanaḥ uvāca tatas dṛṣṭvā eva garuḍam aniruddha śarīra gāḥ śara rūpāḥ mahā sarpāḥ veṣṭayitvā tanum sthitāḥ kṣitim sarve abhyadhāvanta prakṛtyā avasthitāḥ śarāḥ dṛṣṭaḥ spṛṣṭaḥ ca kṛṣṇena saḥ aniruddhaḥ mahātmanā sthitiḥ prīti manāḥ bhūtvā prāñjaliḥ vākyam abravīt bhagavān uvāca āroha garuḍam tūrṇam gacchāma dvārakām purīm tatas te dīrgham adhvānam prayayuḥ puruṣa ṛṣabhāḥ āruhya garuḍam sarve jitvā bāṇam mahā asuram tatas ambara tala sthāḥ te vāruṇīm diśam āśritāḥ velā vana vicāriṇyaḥ nānā varṇāḥ sahasraśas niśamya bāṇagāvaḥ tu tāsu cakre manaḥ tadā āsthitaḥ garuḍam devaḥ sarva loka ādiḥ avyayaḥ āha mām satyabhāmā ca bāṇagāvaḥ mama ānaya yāsām pītvā kila kṣīram na jīryanti mahā asurāḥ atha kārya nirodhaḥ syāt na eva tāsu manaḥ kṛthāḥ dṛśyante gāvaḥ etāḥ tāḥ mām dṛṣṭvā varuṇālayam viśanti sahitāḥ sarvāḥ kāryam atra vidhīyatām tathā iti uktvā tu garuḍaḥ pakṣa vātena sāgaram saḥ avagāḍhaḥ hi sahasā varuṇālayam antikāt tatas tu vāruṇam sainyam abhiyātam su dāruṇam teṣām āpatatām saṃkhye vāruṇānām sahasraśas te bhagnāḥ sahasā yānti tam eva varuṇālayam varuṇena prayuktāni dīpta śastrāṇi saṃyuge tat balam kṛṣṇa bāṇa oghaiḥ dahyamānam samantataḥ saṃstūyamānaḥ bahudhā varuṇaḥ paryavasthitaḥ salila srāviṇā śreṣṭham cāpam udyamya viṣṭhitaḥ sa tu pradhmāpayan śaṅkham varuṇaḥ samadhāvata harim haraḥ iva krodhāt bāṇa jālaiḥ samāvṛṇot bāṇa varṣa ākulāḥ sarvāḥ diśaḥ cakre janārdanaḥ tatas śara oghaiḥ bahudhā varuṇaḥ pīḍitaḥ raṇe smayan iva tadā kṛṣṇam varuṇaḥ pratyayudhyata idam astram mahā ghoram vaiṣṇavam śatru mardanam mayā udyatam vadha artham te tiṣṭha idānīm sthiraḥ bhava vāruṇa astreṇa saṃyojya nanāda sa mahā balaḥ tasya astra vitatāḥ hi āpaḥ varuṇa asya abhiniḥsṛtāḥ vaiṣṇave tu mahā vīrye diśaḥ bhītāḥ pradudruvuḥ smara tām prakṛtim pūrvām avyaktām vyakta lakṣaṇām pañcabhūta āśrayān doṣān ahaṃkāram ca saṃtyaja yā iyam te vaiṣṇavī mūrtiḥ tasyāḥ jyeṣṭhaḥ hi aham tava jyeṣṭha bhāvena mānyaḥ te kim mām dagdhum icchasi pūrvam hi yā tvayā sṛṣṭā prakṛtiḥ vikṛta ātmikā dharmiṇī bīja bhāvena pūrva dharma samāśritā tvayā sṛṣṭam jagat idam sa katham manyase mayi na hi aham prakṛti dveṣī na aham prakṛti dūṣakaḥ vikāraḥ asi vikārāṇām vikāra āyatane anagha rajasā vā api saṃduṣṭā tatas mohaḥ pravartate parāvara jñaḥ sarvajñaḥ aiśvarya vidhim āsthitaḥ gāḥ saṃprayaccha me deva śānti artham bhīma vikrama varuṇaḥ uvāca bāṇena sārdham samayaḥ mayā deva purā kṛtaḥ katham ca samayam kṛtvā kuryām viphalam anyathā jīvan na aham pradāsyāmi gāvaḥ vai vṛṣabha īkṣaṇa hatvā mām naya gāvaḥ tvam eṣa me samayaḥ kṛtaḥ varuṇena evam uktaḥ tu muktvā gāḥ vai mahā yaśāḥ prahasya varuṇam devam mānayām āsa mādhavaḥ tatra devāḥ sa marutaḥ sa sādhyāḥ sa mitiṃjaya durāt eva tu tām dṛṣṭvā dvārakām dvāra mālinīm śrutvā dvāravatī sarvā praharṣam atulam gatā avandanya adavāḥ kṛṣṇam śriyā paramayā yutam saṃstūyamānaḥ govindaḥ dvārakā upari viṣṭhitaḥ tat āścaryam apaśyanta dāśārha gaṇa sattamāḥ bāṇam jitvā mahādevam āyāntam puruṣottamam dvārakā vāsinām vācaḥ caranti bahudhā tadā prāpte kṛṣṇe mahābhāge sātvatānām mahā rathe dhanyāḥ smaḥ anugṛhītāḥ smaḥ yeṣām naḥ jagataḥ patiḥ rakṣitā ca api goptā ca dīrgha bāhuḥ janārdanaḥ prāptaḥ ayam puṇḍarīkākṣaḥ manāṃsi āhlādayan iva evam kathayatām eva dvārakā vāsinām tadā vāsudeva gṛham devāḥ viviśuḥ te mahā rathāḥ tāni teṣām vimānāni divi saṃcaratām tadā sthitāni eva pradṛśyante nānā rūpāṇi sarvaśas siṃha ṛṣabha mṛgaiḥ nāgaiḥ vāji sārasa barhiṇaiḥ sādhyāḥ devāḥ tathā anye ca api avandan tān yathākramam aho vai mahat āścaryam vāsudevasya saṃśrayāt kiranti paurāḥ sarvān tān pūjayantaḥ divaukasaḥ lājaiḥ praṇāmaiḥ dhūpaiḥ ca vāc buddhi niyatāḥ tathā dvārakā vāsinaḥ sarve pūjayanti divaukasaḥ sātyakim ca ulmukam ca eva vipṛthum ca mahā balam etān pariṣvajya tadā mūrdhni ca āghrāya vīryavān andhakam ca śubha akṣam ca tatas vacanam abravīt vāsavaḥ sātvatān sarvān eṣa vaḥ yadu nandanaḥ yojayitvā raṇe ca eva yaśasā pauruṣeṇa ca eṣa bāṇam raṇe jitvā dvārakām punar āgatāḥ sahasra bāhoḥ bāhūnām kṛtvā kṣayam anuttamam sthāpayitvā dvi bāhu tve prāptaḥ ayam sva purīm hariḥ tat api avasitam kṛtsnam naṣṭa śokāḥ vayam kṛtāḥ pibantaḥ madhu mādhvīkam raṃsyāmaḥ prīti saṃyutāḥ kālaḥ yāsyati aviratam viṣayeṣu eva sajja tām bāhūnām saṃśrayāt sarve vayam asya mahātmanaḥ pranaṣṭa śokāḥ raṃsyāmaḥ sarve eva amarāḥ sukham puraṃdaraḥ divam yātaḥ sarva amara gaṇaiḥ vṛtaḥ dvārakām prāpya kṛṣṇaḥ tu reme yadu gaṇaiḥ vṛtaḥ etaiḥ ca kāraṇaiḥ śrīmān vasudeva kule prabhuḥ nivṛtte nārada praśne mayā uktam te samāsatas viṣṇoḥ tu māthure kalpe yatra te saṃśayaḥ mahān vāsudeva gatiḥ ca eva sā mayā samudāhṛtā āścaryaḥ ca eva na anyaḥ asti kṛṣṇaḥ ca āścarya saṃnidhiḥ sarveṣu āścarya kalpeṣu na asti āścaryam avaiṣṇavam vaṃśa pratiṣṭhām atulām śravaṇāt eva lapsyase keśavasya ca māhātmyam na adharmaḥ tān bhajiṣyati eṣā te vaiṣṇavī caryā mayā kārtsnyena kīrtitā āścarya parvam akhilam yaḥ hi idam dhārayet nṛpa sūtaḥ uvāca iti pārikṣitaḥ rājā vaiśaṃpāyana bhāṣitam śrutavān amalaḥ bhūtvā hari vaṃśam dvijarṣabhāḥ evam śaunaka saṃkṣepāt vistareṇa tathā eva ca śaunakaḥ uvāca sūtaḥ uvāca aja śyāmau ca pārśvau tau ubhau api samāhitau tathā eva ca samārūḍhau ajapārśvaḥ tatas abhavat śaunakaḥ uvāca uktaḥ ayam harivaṃśaḥ te parvāṇi nikhilāni ca yathā purā uktāni tathā vyāsa śiṣyeṇa dhīmatā sūtaḥ uvāca tasmin satre samāpte tu rājā pārikṣitaḥ tadā yaṣṭum sa vājimedhena saṃbhārān upacakrame ṛtvij purohita ācāryān āhūya idam uvāca ha tatas asya vijñāya cikīrṣitam tadā kṛṣṇaḥ mahātmā sahasā ājagāma pārikṣitam draṣṭum adīna sattvam dvaipāyanaḥ sarva parāvara jñaḥ pārikṣitaḥ tu nṛpatiḥ dṛṣṭvā tam ṛṣim āgatam tau ca upaviṣṭau abhitas sadasyāḥ tasya śaunaka kathāḥ bahuvidhāḥ citrāḥ cakrāte veda saṃhitāḥ tatas kathā ante nṛpatiḥ codayāmāsa tam munim pitāmaham pāṇḍavānām ātmanaḥ prapitāmaham tvayā tu abhihitam brahman śaṅkhe kṣīram iva āhitam na amṛtena api tṛptiḥ syāt yathā svarga sukhena vā tathā tṛptim na gacchāmi
